Unbounded reward maximization can also cause overoptimization and degrade generation quality. We propose a framework combining Generator-Aligned Reward Modeling (GARM) and Target-Aware Bounded Optimization (TABO). GARM mitigates distribution mismatch through generator-support matching and reduces content confounding using content-matched real contrasts, yielding generator-relevant, attribute-focused reward supervision. TABO maps the learned reward to a smooth, upper-bounded function calibrated from real target-positive examples. This transformation is independent of the optimization algorithm and supports both direct reward-gradient and policy-based optimization. We further introduce Target-Aware GRPO (TA-GRPO), an adaptation of Flow-GRPO that combines fixed stage-wise advantage scaling, a generator-to-target curriculum, and timestep-balanced credit assignment for flow-based video optimization. Experiments show that GARM improves target-attribute expression while maintaining competitive generation quality. TABO better balances attribute enhancement and visual quality than unconstrained reward maximization, while TA-GRPO improves target enhancement over vanilla Flow-GRPO and reduces unintended attribute leakage on neutral prompts. These results highlight the importance of generator-aligned reward supervision and controlled, data-calibrated optimization for targeted visual generation.

</details>


### [39] [ArtCrafter: Feed-Forward Generation of Articulated 3D Object with Analytic Joint Derivation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07658)
*Minh Canh Tu,Quang-Binh Nguyen,Khoi Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ArtCrafter根据物体静止态和打开态两张图像，一次前向生成对应部件网格，并解析计算关节参数，以构建可动3D资产。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人仿真与训练需要可动3D资产，但从普通图像恢复相互一致的部件几何和运动学结构具有挑战性。

Method: 采用基于DiT的生成模型和1+2N槽位布局，联合生成两个状态的部件网格；通过三元注意力保持跨状态几何一致性，并对解码点云施加排斥损失以减少部件穿透；根据对应部件间的刚体变换解析推导URDF关节类型、轴、原点和范围。

Result: 摘要报告，在分布内和分布外数据集上，该方法在相同双状态输入条件下的几何质量和关节运动精度优于现有方法；与不同输入设置或范式的方法相比也具有竞争力。摘要未提供具体数值。

Conclusion: 联合建模双状态部件并解析恢复运动学参数，是生成几何与运动结构一致的可动3D资产的一种有效途径。

Abstract: Articulated 3D assets are essential for robotic simulation and training, yet their generation from casual images poses a key challenge: recovering part geometry and kinematics that remain mutually consistent. We present ArtCrafter, a novel feed-forward generative model for controllable generation of articulated 3D objects from a pair of images, one depicting the object in the rest state and the other in the open state. At the core of ArtCrafter is a DiT-based generative model that jointly produces part-level meshes for both articulation states in a single forward pass. Inspired by Slot Attention, its denoising process operates on a $1+2N$ slot layout comprising one shared base slot and $N$ paired rest/open slots, encouraging each pair to bind to the same kinematic part. To ensure geometrically consistent and disjoint part generation, we introduce Triplet Attention to enforce cross-state geometric consistency between paired rest/open slots anchored through the shared base slot, and a repulsion loss $\mathcal{L}_{\mathrm{rep}}$ on decoded part point clouds to prevent inter-part penetration throughout denoising. Given the resulting paired meshes, URDF joint parameters---type, axis, origin, and range---are derived analytically from the rigid transformation between each rest/open pair, providing an interpretable alternative to learned joint-parameter regression. Extensive experiments on both in-distribution and out-of-distribution datasets demonstrate that ArtCrafter outperforms existing method under the same dual-state setting in geometry quality and articulation precision, while remaining competitive with methods operating under different input settings and paradigms.

</details>


### [40] [Which Objects Stay? Learning Hierarchical Persistence Beliefs for Lifelong Open-Vocabulary Object Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07662)
*Thanh Nguyen Canh,Haolan Zhang,Nak Young Chong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HiPer通过相似实例检索和对象级在线学习，在无需类别标签的条件下估计物体位置持久性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持久性滤波器依赖基于类别的生存先验，难以可靠适用于开放词汇环境。

Method: 利用视觉和几何相似性检索训练实例，根据其观测历史估计初始生存率；以此初始化生存概率的Beta信念，并依据检测和漏检进行闭式更新；物体在新位置被重新识别时，将其持久性信念随之迁移。

Result: 在120条留出仿真序列上，HiPer整体优于对比方法；检索先验优于CLIP标签先验，对象级在线学习进一步提升性能。在47个留出的3RScan环境中，检索先验亦优于先进基线。

Conclusion: 基于数据的检索先验结合对象级在线更新，可改善开放词汇场景下的物体持久性估计；摘要未提供具体性能数值。

Abstract: Lifelong object maps must estimate whether an object remains at its previously observed location after it leaves view. Existing persistence filters rely on class-based survival priors, making them difficult to apply reliably in open-vocabulary settings. We introduce HiPer, a hierarchical persistence filter that derives persistence beliefs directly from data rather than semantic categories. For a new object, HiPer retrieves visually and geometrically similar training instances and estimates an initial survival rate from their observed histories, without requiring class labels. This estimate initializes a conjugate Beta belief over the object's survival probability, which is updated in closed form using detections and missed observations under Rosen's persistence-filter framework. When an object is re-identified at a new location, its persistence belief is transferred with the object, enabling the map to learn object-specific mobility over time. Experiments on 120 held-out simulated sequences show that HiPer consistently achieves the best performance among competing methods. Retrieval-based priors outperform CLIP-derived label priors, and object-specific online learning provides further gains. Results on 47 held-out 3RScan environments demonstrate that the proposed retrieval-based prior also outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in real-world settings.

</details>


### [41] [CoGenRec: A Unified Generative Framework for Complementary Vision Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07665)
*Yapeng Meng,Yuguo Chen,Xiangru Chen,Shaojun Xu,Xuezhen Wang,Lijian Wang,Zheyu Yang,Yihan Lin,Luping Shi,Rong Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoGenRec 利用互补视觉传感器的双路径测量和视频扩散模型，统一实现去模糊、插帧与 HDR 融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高速运动与高动态范围场景受时间分辨率、空间细节、信噪比及动态范围之间的权衡限制，传统相机易出现时间欠采样、运动模糊和传感器饱和。

Method: 融合长曝光、空间稠密而时间稀疏的 RGB 测量，与短曝光、时间稠密而空间稀疏的时空差分测量；通过曝光感知外观建模和双向时间传播提取多尺度时空特征，约束视频扩散模型，并将特征注入 VAE 解码器以恢复细节。

Result: 在 SportsSloMo-CVS 上，相比最强通用基线，去模糊、插帧和 HDR 融合的 PSNR 分别提高 4.40、3.89 和 5.04 dB；在真实 CVS 捕获的 129 个未见场景上亦表现出较好的感知质量。

Conclusion: 互补物理测量与生成式视频先验相结合，为复杂运动和光照条件下的统一高质量重建提供了有效方法。

Abstract: Capturing scenes with extreme motion and high dynamic range is fundamentally constrained by trade-offs among temporal resolution, spatial detail, signal-to-noise ratio, and dynamic range under practical bandwidth, power, and quantum-efficiency limits. Conventional frame-based cameras therefore suffer from temporal undersampling, exposure-induced motion blur, and sensor saturation. The Complementary Vision Sensor (CVS) addresses these limitations by decomposing visual information across two complementary pathways, a spatially dense, temporally sparse, long-exposure RGB pathway and a temporally dense, spatially sparse, short-exposure patiotemporal difference pathway. Their complementary measurements provide appearance, structural, and motion cues across a wide dynamic range that enable reconstruction beyond conventional imaging. We present CoGenRec, a complementarity-driven generative reconstruction framework that unifies video deblurring, high-temporal-resolution frame interpolation, and HDR fusion. CoGenRec encodes complementary measurements into multi-scale spatiotemporal features using exposure-aware appearance modeling and bidirectional temporal propagation. These features constrain a video diffusion model and are injected into the VAE decoder to recover fine structures lost during latent-space compression, combining the scene fidelity of physical measurements with the appearance recovery capabilities of learned video priors. On SportsSloMo-CVS, CoGenRec improves PSNR over the strongest general-purpose baseline by 4.40 dB for deblurring, 3.89 dB for frame interpolation, and 5.04 dB for HDR fusion, respectively. It also achieves strong perceptual quality on 129 unseen scenes captured with real CVS. These results establish a unified approach to advance high-quality reconstruction under challenging motion and illumination conditions. Project page: https://cogenrec.github.io/.

</details>


### [42] [Fast and Accurate Hand Estimation in Pixels, Interaction and World Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07711)
*Jinkun Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FAH通过直接预测手部二维、三维点并拟合参数化网格，实现准确且实时的单目手部重建与追踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法预测骨骼绑定参数后投影网格获取二维关键点；作者发现直接预测的三维关节点与二维预测更一致，基于预测点拟合网格也能降低网格误差。

Method: 基于SAM 3D Body的手部分支，通过微调和特征蒸馏构建模型；预测对应的二维、三维关节点及表面点，拟合MANO或MHR-Hand；可选估计解剖部位和接触，推理时无需物体几何；利用因果细化降低加速度误差，并通过相机估计定位世界坐标中的手部。

Result: 相较所评估基线，图像空间对齐、手部几何、接触估计和世界空间追踪均有改善。RTX 4070 Ti SUPER上，默认ViT-L含网格拟合达65 fps，启用部位和接触估计后为49 fps；最小ViT-S关闭网格拟合、部位和接触估计时达125 fps。

Conclusion: 直接点预测结合参数化网格拟合，可在单目手部重建中兼顾精度、实时性和可扩展功能；摘要未提供各项精度提升的具体数值。

Abstract: Monocular hand mesh estimators commonly predict rig parameters and project the mesh to obtain 2D keypoints. We find that directly predicted 3D joints agree better with 2D predictions, and fitting a mesh to predicted points reduces mesh error. We present FAH (Fast and Accurate Hand Estimation), a family of models built on SAM 3D Body's hand branch through fine-tuning and feature distillation. The models predict corresponding 2D and 3D joints and surface points, fit MANO or MHR-Hand, and optionally estimate anatomical parts and contact without object geometry at inference. Causal refinement reduces acceleration error, and camera estimation places the hands in world space. Experiments show improvements in image-space alignment, hand geometry, contact estimation and world-space tracking over the evaluated baselines. The default ViT-L model runs at 65 fps with mesh fitting on an RTX 4070 Ti SUPER, or 49 fps with parts and contact enabled. The smallest ViT-S model reaches 125 fps without mesh fitting, parts or contact. Code: https://github.com/noahcao/FAH.

</details>


### [43] [Selective Event Evidence and Persistent Temporal Priors for Event-Based Point Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07713)
*Xiaokai Song,Zhang Li,Leqi Liu,Yibin Ye,Kun Wang,Xichao Teng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SEPT通过自适应事件证据加权和跨窗口轨迹先验，提升纯事件相机的长期任意点跟踪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件活动稀疏且随场景和运动变化，导致点对应关系难以建立；长序列中的轨迹估计还容易累积漂移。

Method: 基于ETAP构建SEPT：TEEM仅使用跟踪监督，学习有界可靠性场，在特征提取前沿空间、时间和通道加权事件张量；PTP通过舒尔补边缘化固定局部二次轨迹模型，在重叠窗口间传递几何信息，并利用可见性感知校正和截断末端速度初始化新时间戳坐标。

Result: 模型仅在合成EventKubric上训练，并在五个数据集上评估；相比重新训练的ETAP，SEPT在任意点跟踪平均Jaccard、特征跟踪的特征年龄和预期特征年龄上更优，在EC和EDS上还超过若干事件与帧融合方法。摘要未提供具体数值。

Conclusion: 选择性利用可靠事件证据，并保持跨窗口轨迹约束，是改善纯事件长期点跟踪的有效途径；代码已公开。

Abstract: Event cameras enable tracking under fast motion and challenging illumination. However, stable long-term point tracking remains challenging: event activity is sparse and varies with scene content and motion, complicating correspondence, while trajectory estimates can drift over long sequences. Together, these challenges motivate a tracker that selectively uses informative event evidence and retains trajectory constraints across temporal windows. We propose Selective Event Evidence and Persistent Temporal Priors (SEPT), an event-only framework for tracking any point (TAP) over long sequences. SEPT introduces two complementary modules: Task-Adaptive Event Evidence Modulation (TEEM) and Persistent Trajectory Prior (PTP). Using only tracking supervision, TEEM learns a bounded reliability field that weights the event tensor across space, time, and channels before feature extraction. PTP carries geometric information across overlapping windows by marginalizing a fixed local quadratic trajectory model with a Schur complement. Its visibility-aware correction refines overlap coordinates, while a clipped terminal velocity initializes coordinates at newly exposed timestamps. SEPT builds on the ETAP architecture. SEPT is trained exclusively on synthetic EventKubric data and evaluated on five datasets across TAP and feature tracking. Results show that SEPT outperforms the retrained ETAP baseline in average Jaccard for TAP and in feature age and expected feature age for feature tracking; it also surpasses several methods combining event and frame inputs on EC and EDS. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/sssxxxkkkk/SEPT.

</details>


### [44] [Multimodal Information Interactions Bottleneck for Multimodal Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07718)
*Liangjian Wen,Jiangtao Zheng,Jiang Duan,Qun Dai,Yong Dai,Jianzhuang Liu,Jun Wang,Shu-Dong Huang,Xinwang Liu,Zenglin Xu,Zhao Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MIIB 基于部分信息分解，通过双路径架构和可计算的熵目标学习多模态中的冗余、独有与协同信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态数据存在异质性、信息量不均和复杂交互；对比学习通常假设多视图高度冗余，容易忽略模态独有信息和跨模态协同信息。

Method: 提出多模态信息交互瓶颈（MIIB），采用模态专属编码与多模态融合双路径，并结合变分界和基于分数的熵估计构造代理目标，在无需显式估计互信息的情况下学习最小充分表示。

Result: 摘要称，该方法在多个基准测试中持续优于先进的多模态自监督方法；未提供具体数据或实验设置。

Conclusion: 显式建模多模态信息交互并抑制冗余信息，有助于改善自监督表示；具体效果及适用范围仍需结合完整实验验证。

Abstract: Multimodal self-supervised learning is challenged by heterogeneity, unequal informativeness, and complex information interactions across multimodal data sources such as images, text, audio, and tabular data. While contrastive methods effectively align shared semantics, they typically rely on strong multi-view redundancy assumptions, overlooking modality-specific and synergistic information, thus leading to suboptimal representations. We propose the Multimodal Information Interactions Bottleneck (MIIB), a principled framework grounded in Partial Information Decomposition that explicitly encodes redundant, unique, and synergistic information within multimodal interactions. MIIB employs a dual-path model architecture for modality-specific encoding and multimodal fusion, optimized using surrogate tractable entropy-based objectives that enforce minimal sufficient representations without requiring explicit mutual information estimation. By leveraging variational bounds and score-based entropy estimation, MIIB efficiently captures complex cross-modal interactions while suppressing superfluous information. Experiments across diverse benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements over state-of-the-art multimodal self-supervised methods. Code is available at https://github.com/wanglingzaishi/MIIB.

</details>


### [45] [BiGrounder: Bidirectional Training Unlocks the Potential of GUI Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07765)
*Dengyao Shi,Tianyu Fu,Fei Hu,Anyang Su,Hanning Wang,Shuo Wang,Minghui Wu,Chenxu Zhao,Zhen Lei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出双向奖励与自强化范式，通过生成多样化指令并循环定位，提升GUI定位对语言变化的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练数据中每个目标坐标对应的描述有限，难以覆盖真实场景中的语言变化；模型在强化学习前探索能力不足，也限制了后续性能提升。

Method: 监督微调阶段进行双向训练，使模型既能由指令预测坐标，也能由坐标生成动作描述；强化学习阶段在线策略采样语义一致的指令变体，再将其定位回目标，并使用循环一致性门控将语义质量与定位成功关联起来。

Result: 4B参数模型在五个GUI定位基准上取得有竞争力的准确率；摘要未提供具体数值或对比幅度。

Conclusion: 利用模型自身的指令生成策略持续产生强化学习轨迹，是提升GUI定位能力的有效训练途径。

Abstract: Graphical User Interface (GUI) grounding aims to map natural language instructions to precise operational coordinates, serving as a critical task for autonomous agent interaction. Current approaches formalize GUI grounding as predicting action-coordinate pairs from instructions, with end-to-end training supervised solely by final coordinates. Training datasets typically provide only a small number of descriptions per target coordinate, whereas real-world scenarios involve a much larger set of varied descriptions for the same target, which limits robustness to linguistic variation. While recent GUI grounding paradigms have begun employing reinforcement learning to enhance localization accuracy, models with limited exploratory capability before reinforcement yield constrained improvements even with identical training data during the reinforcement phase. To overcome these limitations, we propose a bidirectional reward paradigm that enables model self-reinforcement. During SFT, bidirectional training equips the model to generate action descriptions from target coordinates. During RL, the model samples semantically consistent instruction variations on-policy and grounds each variation back to its target. A cycle-consistency gate couples semantic quality to grounding success, turning the model's own instruction policy into a source of continually refreshed reinforcement trajectories. Our 4B-parameter model achieves competitive accuracy across five grounding benchmarks in our evaluation, indicating the effectiveness of the proposed training paradigm. https://github.com/Spell-shi/BiGrounder

</details>


### [46] [Rain Beyond Streaks: Cascaded Realistic Rain and Wet Surface Synthesis via Scene Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07793)
*Melissa Tijink,Kirill Tolstobrov,Shunxin Wang,Estefania Talavera,Duc V. Le,Luuk Spreeuwers,Shaodi You,Nicola Strisciuglio*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CascadeRS 利用深度和语义分割先验，联合生成雨线、反射与积水，以构建逼真的雨天场景并支持视觉模型鲁棒性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有雨天合成方法多侧重雨线，忽视反射和积水等表面效应，限制了视觉真实感及下游任务鲁棒性研究。

Method: 构建受物理启发的零样本、可控生成流程，结合深度和语义分割先验，生成与场景几何及语义一致的雨线和表面效应。

Result: 通过大规模用户研究和定量实验验证了生成场景的感知真实感；构建 Cityscapes-CascadeRS 数据集，用于研究雨天条件下的下游任务表现。摘要未提供具体指标。

Conclusion: CascadeRS 为评估和提升视觉系统在雨天的鲁棒性提供了可扩展、逼真的数据生成框架。

Abstract: Realistic rain synthesis is a persistent challenge for vision systems operating in adverse weather. Existing methods primarily focus on generating rain streaks, often neglecting consequent surface effects such as reflections and puddles, which are critical for perceptual realism and robustness of downstream vision tasks. We introduce CascadeRS, a rain synthesis framework that leverages depth and segmentation priors to render scene-consistent rain and surface effects. In addition to rain streaks, CascadeRS produces reflections and puddles aligned with scene semantics through a physics-inspired zero-shot, controllable generative pipeline. It produces photorealistic rainy urban scenes, with high perceptual realism, which we validate qualitatively with an extensive user study and quantitatively. We produce the Cityscapes-CascadeRS dataset, to study downstream task performance under real rainy conditions. Our results establish CascadeRS as a scalable and realistic rain-generation framework for evaluating and enhancing the robustness of vision in adverse rainy weather. Code and dataset are available via https://github.com/nis-research/CascadeRainSynthesis

</details>


### [47] [Self-Supervised Diffusion Models for Handwritten Signature Verification via Ruin Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07810)
*Peirong Zhang,Zhenhua Yang,Jiaxin Zhang,Moises Diaz,Miguel A. Ferrer,Umapada Pal,Wei Zhou,Lianwen Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SigDiffuser利用严重破坏后的签名重建作为自监督任务，结合扩散模型学习更具判别性的手写签名表示，并在多语言数据集上取得优于现有方法的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手写签名验证的效果依赖于表示的判别能力，而现有方法主要依赖监督学习，自监督学习在该领域的潜力尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 提出基于扩散模型的自监督框架SigDiffuser。其“从废墟中重建”（RFR）任务通过严重揉皱破坏签名结构与书写风格，由视觉编码器从完好区域提取笔迹特征，引导去噪器重建受损部分；金字塔特征聚合模块（PFAM）通过分层聚合和调制连接编码器与去噪器，提升表示质量。

Result: 在多语言签名数据集上的实验表明，该方法优于最先进的自监督学习方法和手写签名验证模型；提高揉皱严重程度可改善表示质量。摘要未提供具体指标或数值。

Conclusion: 极端破坏与自监督扩散重建相结合，可以比传统轻度变形学习到更优的签名表示，并可能为其他生物特征识别任务提供思路。

Abstract: Handwritten signature verification (HSV) is a critical biometric technique in legal and forensic authentication, whose efficacy hinges on the discriminability of signature representations. Current HSV methods predominantly adopt supervised learning to enhance representation quality, leaving self-supervised learning largely unexplored. We propose \emph{SigDiffuser}, a diffusion-based self-supervised framework for signature representation and verification. At its core, \emph{SigDiffuser} introduces a novel pretext task termed \emph{Reconstruction from Ruins (RFR)}, which deliberately ``ruins'' signatures through severe crumpling, obliterating their structural integrity and writing styles. A visual encoder is employed to capture crucial handwriting patterns from intact signature regions, guiding the denoiser to reconstruct the ``ruined'' portions and learning meaningful representations. Additionally, we introduce a \emph{pyramidal feature aggregation module (PFAM)} to bridge the visual encoder and denoiser, enhancing representation quality via hierarchical aggregation and modulation. Extensive experiments on multilingual signature datasets demonstrate that \emph{SigDiffuser} significantly outperforms both state-of-the-art SSL methods and HSV models. We further show that increasing the crumpling severity on signatures effectively improves representation quality. Notably, our work demonstrates that extreme destruction, coupled with self-supervised diffusion models, can yield superior signature representation compared to traditional mild deformations, paving new avenues for HSV and other biometric domains. Code will be publicly available.

</details>


### [48] [MV-Sign: Multi-View 3D Motion Integration and Enhanced Text-to-Motion Conditioning for Sign Language Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07811)
*Singyu Muk,Kaustubh Kundu,Kunyuan Xie,Weijie Wang,Kalin Stefanov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文通过多视角高质量三维数据和增强文本条件的生成模型，提升手语动作生成精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有三维手语生成方法依赖单视角正面视频拟合的伪三维数据，受自遮挡和手部细节缺失影响，且文本条件约束不足。

Method: 构建跨视角融合与非手语片段过滤的数据集 How2Sign-MV3D；采用手语感知时空 VQ-VAE 联合编码身体与手部动作，并结合伪 gloss 适配器和关键帧感知检索改善文本与动作对齐。

Result: 在 How2Sign 基准上，相比最先进方法，身体和手部 DTW-PA-JPE 分别降低 32.8% 和 20.9%。

Conclusion: 多视角数据与优化文本条件路径有助于提高三维手语生成质量；作者还发布 ASLCitizen-3D、MM-WAuslan-MV3D 和多视角视频基准 SGNify-MV。

Abstract: Sign language production (SLP) aims to generate appropriate sign language motion from spoken language text. However, current 3D SLP methods suffer from noisy supervision and weak text conditioning. Specifically, these methods typically rely on pseudo-3D motion data fitted from single-view frontal videos, which introduces severe self-occlusion and loses fine-grained hand details, ultimately degrading downstream production. To overcome these limitations, we propose a comprehensive framework that jointly integrates multi-view 3D motion data and optimizes the text-conditioning pathway. First, we introduce How2Sign-MV3D, a high-fidelity dataset constructed via a novel cross-view curation pipeline that fuses multi-view estimates to resolve occlusions and filter non-signing segments. Second, we design a robust SLP model incorporating a sign-aware spatio-temporal VQ-VAE (ST-VQVAE) for joint body-hand tokenization, a pseudo-gloss adapter, and keyframe-aware retrieval to enhance text-to-motion alignment. Evaluated on the How2Sign benchmark, our framework reduces body and hand DTW-PA-JPE by 32.8% and 20.9% respectively compared to the state-of-the-art. Finally, we release two new high-quality 3D datasets, ASLCitizen-3D and MM-WAuslan-MV3D, alongside SGNify-MV as a multi-view video benchmark to support broader community research.

</details>


### [49] [CMDiff: Left Atrial Scar Segmentation from 3D LGE MRI Using Conditional Mask Diffusion Model: A Feasibility Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07814)
*Jing Zhang,Bastien Bergere,Jonas Leite,Emilie Bollache,Mikaël Laredo,Alban Redheuil,Nadjia Kachenoura*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 条件掩膜扩散可用于左心房瘢痕分割，但在本研究中不及确定性基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LGE心脏MRI中的左心房瘢痕与房颤消融结果相关，但低对比度、心房壁薄、标注稀缺及类别极度不平衡使自动分割困难；条件扩散对稀疏三维瘢痕分割的适用性尚不明确。

Method: 提出CMDiff，使用动态编码器条件化的三维正弦UNet直接预测干净掩膜。基于60例公开LASCARQS标注病例进行五折交叉验证，每折48例训练、12例留出测试，并在相同病例上与确定性基线比较。

Result: CMDiff的DSC为0.47±0.08、HD95为12.89±6.39 mm、ASD为1.91±0.96 mm；基线分别为0.51±0.08、10.86±5.39 mm和1.58±1.03 mm。同一实现下，干净掩膜预测明显优于所评估的噪声预测配置。

Conclusion: CMDiff技术上可行，但相对于基线DSC更低、边界距离误差更高，未带来性能提升；直接掩膜扩散是可供分析的替代方案，而非该任务上具有竞争力的性能改进。

Abstract: Late gadolinium enhancement (LGE) cardiac MRI enables non-invasive assessment of left atrial (LA) scar, an imaging marker associated with atrial fibrillation ablation outcome. However, automatic scar segmentation is hindered by low contrast, the thin atrial wall, scarce labels, and extreme class imbalance. Conditional diffusion offers iterative mask generation, but its suitability for sparse 3D LA scar segmentation remains unclear. To address this gap, we propose Conditional Mask Diffusion (CMDiff), which predicts clean masks using a dynamic-encoder-conditioned 3D sinusoidal UNet. We compared CMDiff with a deterministic baseline on identical cases in five-fold cross-validation of 60 scar-labelled public LASCARQS subjects, with 48 training and 12 held-out cases per fold. CMDiff achieved a scar DSC of 0.47 $\pm$ 0.08, HD95 of 12.89 $\pm$ 6.39 mm, and ASD of 1.91 $\pm$ 0.96 mm, compared with deterministic baseline values of 0.51 $\pm$ 0.08, 10.86 $\pm$ 5.39 mm, and 1.58 $\pm$ 1.03 mm, respectively. Under the same implementation, clean-mask prediction performed substantially better than the noise-prediction configuration evaluated in this study. Thus, CMDiff was technically feasible but did not improve segmentation performance: DSC was lower, and both HD95 and ASD were higher. Direct mask diffusion therefore provides an analytical alternative, but not a competitive performance advance for this task. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/jizhang02/CMDiff.

</details>


### [50] [STAG-Net: Structure-Aware Attention over Landmark Groups for Lightweight Head Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07828)
*Asiri Gawesha Lindamulage,Sanka Mohottala,Dharshana Kasthurirathna,Nuwan Kodagoda*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: STAG-Net 利用面部关键点实现轻量级头部姿态估计，在多个评测设置下兼顾领先精度与低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图像的头部姿态估计方法计算成本较高，难以部署于资源受限环境。

Method: 基于面部关键点，结合运动感知筛选、空间聚类、紧凑 DeepSet 编码器、自注意力及全局几何描述符；构建 FaceMesh 和 FAN 两种变体。

Result: 在所有评测设置中，STAG-Net 的平均绝对误差均为所比较关键点方法中最低；BIWI 有效检测帧上，FAN 变体跨数据集和受试者独立评测分别达到 3.51° 和 2.39°。回归器包含 0.012–0.043M 参数、24.1–83.6K FLOPs；FaceMesh 完整流水线在 Tesla T4 上达到 322.9 FPS，CPU 上达到 111.0 FPS。

Conclusion: 该方法在关键点方法中表现出领先精度，同时显著降低训练与推理成本；端到端速度仍依赖关键点提取器和运行硬件。

Abstract: Head pose estimation is important for human computer interaction and driver monitoring, but many image based methods remain too costly for resource constrained deployment. We propose STAG-Net, a structure-aware attention over landmark groups for lightweight head Pose estimation that estimates head pose from facial landmarks using movement aware selection, spatial clustering, compact DeepSet encoders, self attention, and a global geometry descriptor. We develop variants using FaceMesh and Face Alignment Network landmarks and evaluate them under cross dataset and subject independent protocols on 300W-LP, AFLW2000, and BIWI. STAG-Net achieves the lowest mean absolute error among all compared landmark based methods in every evaluated setting. On BIWI frames with valid detections, the FAN variant also records the lowest overall error among all compared methods, reaching $3.51^\circ$ under cross dataset evaluation and $2.39^\circ$ under subject independent evaluation. The regressors use only 0.012 to 0.043 million parameters and 24.1 to 83.6 thousand FLOPs, about three orders of magnitude fewer FLOPs than FSA-Net. Despite including landmark extraction and regression, the FaceMesh pipeline reaches 322.9 frames per second on a Tesla T4 GPU, nearly twice the 164.5 frames per second of the fastest measured image based baseline, and 111.0 frames per second on CPU. After normalization by dataset size, STAG-Net processes training samples seven to eight times faster than FSA-Net and more than one hundred times faster than HopeNet and TokenHPE, while complete training finishes within 28 minutes. These results show that STAG-Net combines leading landmark based accuracy with substantially lower training and inference cost.

</details>


### [51] [THG-Omni: Traceable Human-Centric Graph Supervision for Video Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07836)
*Jisheng Dang,Kai Wei,Ronghao Lin,Bimei Wang,Hong Peng,Wei-Shi Zheng,Bin Hu,Qi Tian,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: THG-Omni通过以人为中心的时空图监督，将视频推理答案与可识别的参与者和事件关联，并在多个基准上提升表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全模态视频问答模型通常无法明确指出答案所依据的参与者、事件或时间区间；仅使用答案级监督也不足以训练模型理解结构化视频证据。

Method: 结合Segment Anything Model 3的参与者轨迹与Qwen3-VL的时间定位标注，构建人本图监督。候选语义陈述经源视频及可用音频核验后用于生成训练问题，轨迹几何信息提供时空监督；推理时使用从评估视频预测的图。

Result: 在IntentBench的社会推理、情绪理解和欺骗感知任务上，相较未经适配的骨干模型均有提升。NExT-QA上，匹配图输入准确率为89.54%，无图输入为85.10%。另一个验证实验中，紧凑图、完整图和无图输入分别为78.22%、78.02%和77.58%。诊断实验表明，即使没有源视频和音频，预测图仍保留有助于回答问题的证据。

Conclusion: 结构化图监督可改善人本视频推理，并将证据关联到可识别的参与者与事件；摘要未提供完整实验设置，不宜直接比较不同实验中的绝对准确率。

Abstract: Omni-modal models can answer questions about video, but their predictions rarely show which participant, event, or interval supports an answer. Moreover, answer-level supervision alone does not teach a model to interpret structured video evidence. To systematically mitigate this issue, we present THG-Omni, a human-centric graph-supervision framework that links Segment Anything Model~3 participant tracks with temporally grounded Qwen3-VL annotations. Candidate semantic claims are checked against the source video and available audio before they are used to construct training questions, while track geometry provides explicit spatial and temporal supervision. The resulting questions teach an omni-modal reasoning model to interpret participant attributes, actions, affect, interactions, and their timing from a structured graph. At inference, THG-Omni uses a predicted graph constructed from the evaluation video. Experiments on IntentBench show consistent improvements over the unadapted backbone across social reasoning, emotion understanding, and deception perception. On NExT-QA, THG-Omni achieves 89.54\% accuracy with the matched graph, compared with 85.10\% without graph input. Further validation experiments show that compact graph input achieves 78.22\% accuracy, compared with 78.02\% using the full graph and 77.58\% without graph input. A complementary diagnostic demonstrates that the predicted graph preserves evidence useful for answering questions even without the source video and audio. Together, these findings show that THG-Omni improves human-centric video reasoning while grounding its evidence in identifiable participants and events. Our codes are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/review-n94hhfcwp5666/

</details>


### [52] [Enhanced MRI brain tumor detection using deep learning in conjunction with explainable AI SHAP based diverse and multi feature analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07843)
*Asif Rahman,Maqsood Hayat,Nadeem Iqbal,Fawaz Khaled Alarfaj,Salem Alkhalaf,Fahad Alturise*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究比较多种MRI特征表示与分类算法，发现LBP结合SVC或CNN具有较高诊断性能；大规模基准数据集上CNN准确率为98.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统诊断方法存在分辨率、辐射暴露或对比度方面的局限，研究旨在利用MRI及机器学习提高脑肿瘤识别的准确性和自动化水平。

Method: 比较LBP、Gabor滤波、离散小波变换、快速傅里叶变换、CNN特征和灰度游程矩阵等表示方式，以及K近邻、随机森林、SVC、PNN和CNN；通过卡方与p值检验评估LBP特征的统计意义，并用SHAP分析特征重要性。

Result: 小数据集上CNN与SVC准确率分别为97.8%和98.06%；大型基准数据集上分别为98.9%和96.7%。摘要称LBP与SVC、CNN组合具有较高特异度和准确率，但未提供特异度数值。

Conclusion: CNN在大型基准数据集上表现最佳，LBP特征对分类具有潜在价值；摘要未说明数据划分、外部验证及置信区间，因此尚不足以判断临床泛化能力。

Abstract: Recent innovations in medical imaging have markedly improved brain tumor identification, surpassing conventional diagnostic approaches that suffer from low resolution, radiation exposure, and limited contrast. Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI) is pivotal in precise and accurate tumor characterization owing to its high-resolution, non-invasive nature. This study investigates the synergy among multiple feature representation schemes such as local Binary Patterns (LBP), Gabor filters, Discrete Wavelet Transform, Fast Fourier Transform, Convolutional Neural Networks (CNN), and Gray-Level Run Length Matrix alongside five learning algorithms namely: k-nearest Neighbor, Random Forest, Support Vector Classifier (SVC), and probabilistic neural network (PNN), and CNN. Empirical findings indicate that LBP in conjunction with SVC and CNN obtained high specificity and accuracy, rendering it a promising method for MRI-based tumor diagnosis. Further to investigate the contribution of LBP, Statistical analysis chi-square and p-value tests are used to confirm the significant impact of LBP feature space for identification of brain Tumor. In addition, The SHAP analysis was used to identify the most important features in classification. In a small dataset, CNN obtained 97.8% accuracy while SVC yielded 98.06% accuracy. In subsequent analysis, a large benchmark dataset is also utilized to evaluate the performance of learning algorithms in order to investigate the generalization power of the proposed model. CNN achieves the highest accuracy of 98.9%, followed by SVC at 96.7%. These results highlight CNN's effectiveness in automated, high-precision tumor diagnosis. Code: https://github.com/asifrahman557/BrainTumorDetection

</details>


### [53] [ATHAAR: A Medieval Scientific Arabic Manuscript Dataset for Handwritten Text Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08091)
*Ismail El Jamiy,Khadija Msalek,Guillaume Loizelet,Farah Benamara,Nabil Hathout*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出面向中世纪阿拉伯语科学手稿的转写规范与人工标注数据集 ATHAAR，并评估视觉语言模型的跨域迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连笔、变动的附加符号及训练数据匮乏，使现有模型难以识别中世纪阿拉伯语科学手稿。

Method: 借鉴文本校勘学制定转写标注规范；构建涵盖同一文本不同传本、书写风格及文字和数字内容的人工标注数据集；使用先进视觉语言模型开展跨书写风格、章节和著作的迁移实验。

Result: 实验表明，当前系统在处理古代科学文本的特殊性时存在局限；摘要未提供具体量化指标。

Conclusion: 专门的标注规范和多样化数据集为该任务提供了研究基础，但现有视觉语言模型仍不足以充分应对这类手稿。

Abstract: Despite recent progress in handwritten text recognition, medieval Arabic scientific manuscripts remain an open challenge for current vision-language models (VLMs) due to cursive ligatures, variable diacritization, and more importantly the lack of training data. This paper aims to address this gap, by proposing the first annotation guidelines dedicated to the transcription of scientific Arabic manuscripts inspired by textual criticism, a well-established approach to historical text editing. We then propose, ATHAAR, a new dataset manually annotated following this guideline. Unlike previous resources in the field, ATHAAR varies both witnesses of the same text, calligraphic styles, and content to deal with texts but also numerals. Relying on state-of-the-art VLMs, we finally design a set of experiments to study models transfer across scripts, chapters, and treatises. Our findings highlight the limitations of current systems when faced with the specificities of ancient scientific texts.

</details>


### [54] [StitchEdit: Stitching Depth Priors into Image Editors via Per-Layer Gradient Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07922)
*Qin Guo,Dan Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StitchEdit通过探测编辑与深度估计目标在不同网络层的关系，选择性迁移几何先验，提升依赖三维结构的图像编辑效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在空间变换、遮挡调整和视角变化等任务中缺乏可靠的三维结构理解；尽管预训练骨干包含几何先验，直接使用深度条件仍可能忽略几何或降低视觉质量。

Method: 先利用轻量级深度去噪适配器激活骨干的几何能力，再依据逐层梯度探测结果设置分层学习率，对适配器进行编辑微调：保留有益的深度知识，削弱与编辑目标冲突的知识。探测发现两个目标在Transformer早期层相容、在后期层冲突。

Result: 在包含150项几何依赖编辑任务的StitchEditBench上，该方法在感知质量和几何保真度方面持续优于指令式及深度条件基线；摘要未提供具体数值。

Conclusion: 按层识别并调节深度估计与图像编辑的目标冲突，有助于将预训练扩散模型的几何先验有效用于空间编辑。

Abstract: Text-guided image editing with diffusion models struggles with spatial edits (e.g. manipulating objects, changing viewpoints, manipulating occlusions) that depend on the 3D structure understanding of a scene. Marigold and follow-up work demonstrate that a pretrained diffusion backbone already harbors strong geometric priors that can be activated for depth estimation, yet how to redirect these priors toward editing remains unresolved. Through per-layer gradient probing, we discover that the editing and depth objectives are compatible in early transformer layers but actively conflict in late layers, explaining why naive depth-conditioned editing either ignores geometry or degrades visual quality. We present StitchEdit, which first activates the backbone's geometric capability via a lightweight depth-denoising adapter, then selectively fine-tunes this adapter for editing using a probe-guided per-layer learning rate schedule that preserves depth knowledge where it helps and prunes it where it conflicts. On the proposed StitchEditBench, a benchmark of 150 geometry-dependent edits spanning spatial transformation, occlusion manipulation, and viewpoint change, StitchEdit consistently surpasses both instruction-based and depth-conditioned baselines on perceptual quality and geometric fidelity.

</details>


### [55] [Towards Scalable Diagnostic-Driven Whole-Slide Image Retrieval for Pathology Archives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07935)
*Jingsong Liu,Youssef Wally Han Li,Weiwei Zhou,Jing Dai,Elisabeth Wetzer,Peter J. Schüffler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CLEAR-WSI 利用固定长度的全切片图像嵌入和诊断标签精排，实现可扩展的病理图像检索，在四项任务中均优于所评估的最强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字病理全切片图像档案迅速增长，但元数据不一致或缺失，导致病例检索困难；现有方法依赖手工索引和预定义采样，限制了可扩展性与泛化能力。

Method: 将全切片图像表示为固定长度嵌入，按视觉相似性检索候选病例，并根据检索场景使用预测诊断标签或真实诊断标签精排；将新处理病例加入持续扩展的记忆库。

Result: 在两个公开数据集和一个私人临床数据集构成的四项任务中，相对最强基线的 Precision@5 增益分别为 CAMELYON16 3.10、BRACS-1 2.75、BRACS-2 1.95、X-HER2 3.75；摘要未明确增益单位。消融和延迟研究进一步评估了组件贡献与部署潜力。

Conclusion: 该框架展示了诊断驱动的全切片检索性能及扩展潜力，但摘要未提供具体延迟、统计显著性或真实标签可用性等细节。

Abstract: The growing adoption of digital pathology has rapidly expanded collections of hematoxylin-and-eosin whole-slide images, yet efficient navigation through these archives remains challenging, as metadata is often inconsistent or unavailable. Content-based image retrieval addresses this challenge by identifying similar cases directly from visual representations instead of manual annotations or metadata. Existing retrieval systems have demonstrated promising performance but largely rely on handcrafted indexing pipelines and predefined sampling strategies, constraining scalability and generalization. Here, we present CLEAR-WSI (Constant-Length Embedding for Automatic Retrieval of Whole-Slide Images) as a scalable, diagnostic-driven slide-retrieval framework, validated on both public and clinical data. Using a fixed-length slide embedding, CLEAR-WSI refines retrieved candidates with either a predicted diagnostic label or the ground-truth label, depending on the retrieval setting. Newly processed cases are subsequently added to the expanding memory bank, allowing the retrieval archive to grow over time. On a comprehensive benchmark consisting of two public and one private clinical datasets representing four retrieval tasks, CLEAR-WSI improves Precision@5 over the strongest evaluated baseline, with gains on CAMELYON16 (3.10), BRACS-1 (2.75), BRACS-2 (1.95), and X-HER2 (3.75). Additional ablation and latency studies further examine the contribution of each component and highlight its potential for scalable deployment.

</details>


### [56] [ProQuant: Progressive Quantization-aware Training for Edge MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08216)
*Yufei Xue,Yushi Huang,Jiawei Shao,Pingcheng Dong,Yonghao Tan,Shiyao Li,Kwang-Ting Cheng,Jun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ProQuant 是面向低比特多模态大语言模型的量化感知训练框架，通过自重标注和渐进式模块激活缓解 W4A8 量化带来的性能损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型计算与内存开销大，难以部署于资源受限的边缘设备；训练后量化在 W4A8 等低比特设置下性能显著下降，尤其影响适合边缘部署的小模型。

Method: 采用权重与激活量化感知训练；使用自重标注（SRL）重新采样开源多模态数据集的回答，以缩小微调数据与预训练数据的分布差异；解耦多模态理解与语言建模能力，利用渐进式模块激活（PBA）优先恢复多模态理解，并通过视觉敏感因子 τ 识别多模态关键层。

Result: 在涵盖 2B–8B 参数模型的多个基准上，ProQuant 优于现有方法。W4A8 Qwen3-VL-2B-Instruct 的平均准确率提升 6.6%，相较全精度模型性能仅下降约 1%；摘要未明确提升的基线及百分比是否为百分点。

Conclusion: ProQuant 表明，结合数据分布对齐与针对多模态关键层的渐进恢复，可有效改善低比特量化模型的性能，为边缘部署提供可行方案；具体资源节省和训练成本未在摘要中给出。

Abstract: Multi-modal large language models (MLLMs) with impressive perception and understanding capabilities will enable a range of downstream applications. However, their substantial computational and memory requirements hinder the real-world implementation, particularly for resource-constrained edge devices. Quantization has emerged as an effective solution for deploying large models. While post-training quantization (PTQ) successfully retains the performance at moderate bit-width (e.g., W8A8), it suffers from severe performance degradation under low-bit quantization (e.g., W4A8), especially for small-scale models that are more suitable for edge deployment. In this paper, we propose ProQuant, a novel weight-activation quantization-aware training (QAT) framework tailored for low-bit MLLMs. First, to align the limited finetuning dataset distribution with the pretrained dataset distribution, we propose a self-relabeling (SRL) scheme to resample the responses of the open-source multimodal dataset. Second, we are the first to take a decoupled view of multi-modal (MM) understanding and language modeling abilities. We propose a progressive block activation (PBA) mechanism to prioritize the MM understanding recovery. We also introduce a vision-sensitive factor $\vecτ$ that effectively identifies the MM-critical layers. Extensive experiments across various benchmarks, covering models with 2B-8B parameters, show that ProQuant significantly outperforms existing methods. For example, our W4A8 Qwen3-VL-2B-Instruct improves average accuracy by impressively 6.6% and achieves performance comparable to full precision counterparts with only approximately 1% performance drop. Code is available at https://github.com/yufeixue-ai/ProQuant/.

</details>


### [57] [WAM-Sim0.1: World Anchored Memory Meets Online RL for Consistent and Realistic Driving Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07939)
*Jiahao Cui,Jiaye Li,Wenhao Zhang,Yaoyi Li,Shan Luan,Quanhui Tang,Jianhua Han,Lin Cheng,Hang Xu,Siyu Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WAM-Sim通过持久化分层记忆与在线流匹配强化学习，缓解自动驾驶世界模型长时生成中的结构漂移、身份漂移和画质衰退。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归驾驶世界模型依赖缺乏持久状态的瞬时条件信号，长期滚动生成时容易丢失道路结构与交通参与者身份，并出现视觉质量下降。

Method: 构建三层持久记忆：位姿对齐的结构令牌编码道路拓扑，只读场景令牌提供地理定位的背景外观，一次写入的实例令牌维持交通参与者身份；对模型自主生成的轨迹进行在线流匹配强化学习后训练，结合层级对齐视觉奖励与视觉语言模型物理评判。

Result: 在nuScenes上，FID为3.47、FVD为25.72；下游感知达到24.16 mAP和71.23道路mIoU。自采数据的分钟级滚动实验显示，结构和身份漂移得到明显缓解。

Conclusion: 持久分层记忆为长期生成提供稳定上下文，在线强化学习进一步改善物理合理性和视觉保真度；摘要未提供详细消融实验或对比基线。

Abstract: Autoregressive driving world models systematically suffer from structural drift, identity drift, and quality decay over long rollout horizons due to transient conditioning signals that lack persistent state. We present WAM-Sim, a driving world model anchored by a persistent hierarchical memory and optimized via online flow-matching RL. WAM-Sim structures persistent context across three temporal and semantic scales: pose-aligned structural tokens encoding persistent road topology, read-only scene tokens supplying geolocated background appearance, and write-once instance tokens preserving individual traffic participant identities across occlusions and chunk boundaries. To restore visual sharpness and physical plausibility degraded by multi-scale conditioning, we introduce an online post-training framework that optimizes self-generated rollouts via flow-matching RL guided by hierarchy-aligned visual rewards and a vision-language model physical judge. The invariant memory empirically confines policy optimization within the anchored context, helping suppress semantic collapse and reward hacking, while online RL systematically enforces physical commonsense and fine-grained visual fidelity. On the nuScenes benchmark, WAM-Sim achieves a superior generation fidelity (FID=3.47, FVD=25.72) and strong downstream perception performance (24.16 mAP, 71.23 road mIoU). On minute-scale rollouts of self-collected data, WAM-Sim substantially mitigates structural and identity drift, preserving high scene similarity and subject identity coherence across extended horizons. Project page: https://fudan-generative-vision.github.io/WAM-Sim.

</details>


### [58] [IDPrior: Decoupling Identity Generation from Facial Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07941)
*Fadi Boutros,Meriç Demirörs,Darian Tomašević,Vitomir Štruc,Naser Damer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: IDPrior通过在预训练人脸识别模型的嵌入空间中学习身份分布，生成新身份并合成对应人脸图像，以构建用于人脸识别训练的合成数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实身份标注人脸数据的采集与标注成本高，且涉及隐私和伦理问题；现有合成方法通常未显式建模人脸身份的潜在分布。

Method: 采用两阶段框架：首先在预训练人脸识别模型的嵌入空间中学习扩散先验，生成新身份嵌入；随后以该嵌入作为条件，使用预训练的身份条件扩散模型生成同一身份的多张图像。

Result: 实验显示，生成的身份具有多样性和良好的类间区分度，其分布更接近真实身份分布；使用所生成数据训练的人脸识别模型在多个验证基准上达到与现有合成数据方法相当或更优的性能。摘要未提供具体数值。

Conclusion: 显式学习身份分布并将身份生成与图像合成解耦，可为人脸识别提供可扩展的合成数据方案；模块化设计兼容现有及未来的身份条件扩散模型。

Abstract: Large-scale identity-labeled face datasets are essential for training modern face recognition models, yet collecting and annotating such data is costly and raises privacy and ethical concerns. Existing synthetic face generation methods typically derive identities from predefined class labels, unconditional image generation, or predefined latent sampling strategies, without explicitly modeling the underlying distribution of facial identities. In this paper, we propose IDPrior, a two-stage framework that decouples identity generation from image synthesis. We first learn a diffusion prior in the embedding space of a pretrained face recognition model to model the distribution of facial identities and generate novel identity embeddings. These embeddings are then used to condition a pretrained identity-conditioned diffusion model (DM), enabling the synthesis of multiple images of the same identity. This modular design provides a scalable approach to identity generation while remaining compatible with existing and future identity-conditioned DMs. Experiments indicate that IDPrior generates diverse and well-separated identities while maintaining a closer correspondence to the distribution of authentic identities. Face recognition models trained on datasets generated using IDPrior-generated identities achieve competitive or improved performance compared with existing synthetic-data-based methods across multiple face verification benchmarks. Code and generated datasets are publicly available at https://github.com/fdbtrs/IDperturb.

</details>


### [59] [Rotation Is Token-Blind: A Structural Criterion for Post-Training Quantization of Feed-Forward Geometry Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07955)
*Huanfei He,Bohao Zhang,Jiaxin Zhang,Wei Sui,John See,Zeyd Boukhers,Cong Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: QMapAnything 是面向前馈式三维重建 Transformer 的结构感知训练后量化框架，在降低部署开销的同时保持接近全精度的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长序列和深层注意力导致计算与内存开销高；不同类型的 token 共用激活量化网格，特殊 token 的量化误差可能因其全局输出影响力而被放大。

Method: 将保持浮点计算结果不变的旋转与平滑视为 token 无差别重参数化，分析其对相对 token 信噪比的局限；提出结合特殊 token 量化劣势与输出影响力的统计量 Γ，判断旋转之外是否需要差异化偏差校正。

Result: 几何基准上，W4A8 性能接近全精度；W8A8 相比 FP32 的峰值内存降低 2.2–3.1 倍，真实硬件加速最高达 3.1 倍。

Conclusion: 结构感知的训练后量化为资源受限边缘设备部署前馈式三维重建模型提供了有效途径；摘要未给出具体基准数值或消融细节。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D models use Transformers to predict camera poses, depth, and structure in one pass, avoiding iterative multi-view optimization. Long sequences and deep attention incur high compute and memory costs, motivating Post-Training Quantization (PTQ) for deployment. Existing PTQ methods mainly suppress channel-wise outliers by smoothing or orthogonal rotation. Geometry Transformers introduce a different challenge: heterogeneous token types share one activation quantization grid. A few special tokens may exert high leverage over global outputs and be updated by only a subset of blocks. Token-blind transforms, which apply the same transformation to every token, reduce overall quantization error but cannot remove differentiated degradation across token types. We propose QMapAnything, a structure-aware PTQ framework. We cast floating-point-preserving rotation and smoothing as a token-blind reparameterization group, and prove that they yield common-mode gains while leaving relative token SNR unchanged. We introduce a statistic $Γ$ that combines a special token's quantization disadvantage and output leverage to predict whether differentiated bias correction is needed beyond rotation. Experiments on geometric benchmarks show that W4A8 closely matches full-precision performance. Under W8A8, peak memory falls by $2.2$--$3.1\times$ versus FP32, with up to $3.1\times$ speedup on real hardware. These results provide a principled and efficient approach to deploying feed-forward 3D reconstruction models in resource-constrained edges.

</details>


### [60] [Bri3D: Bridging the Object-Scene Gap in Single-Image 3D Generation via Scene-Anchor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07961)
*Hongye Hou,Shuai Zhang,Hongjie Li,Zhuoxiao Li,Haiping Wang,Yuan Liu,Wufan Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Bri3D 利用实例感知的体素场景锚点，结合场景级布局推理与预训练对象生成先验，从单张图像生成布局一致、几何细致的组合式 3D 场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在场景级监督有限时难以协调多个对象；将对象生成器直接扩展到整场景还可能损害几何质量，并无法保持实例的尺度、朝向和姿态。

Method: 先预测以颜色区分实例的粗粒度体素场景骨架，编码对象几何和空间布局；再提取各实例作为场景锚点，将其细化为保持姿态且兼容生成先验的体素支撑；最后使用冻结的预训练对象生成器，在锚点定义的空间参考中生成带纹理的精细几何。

Result: 摘要报告该方法在场景布局、对象几何、视觉质量和泛化能力方面均有所提升；完整场景生成速度快于 SceneGen 和 SAM3D。摘要未提供具体量化结果。

Conclusion: 通过粗到细地分离场景组织与对象细节生成，Bri3D 能复用预训练对象先验，并将场景级训练集中于空间组织和锚点细化，从而改善单图 3D 场景生成的质量与效率。

Abstract: Generating compositional 3D scenes from a single image requires recovering detailed object geometry and coherent spatial arrangements. Existing object-to-scene methods, including SAM3D and MIDI, extend object generation through image-context reasoning or inter-instance modeling. Coordinating objects remains challenging with limited scene-level supervision. Directly extending object-centric generators to entire scenes can degrade geometry and fail to preserve instance scale, orientation, and pose. We propose Bri3D, a framework bridging scene-level structural reasoning and object-level generative priors through instance-aware voxel scene anchors. Bri3D predicts a color-coded scene scaffold encoding coarse object geometry and spatial arrangements. Each instance is extracted as a scene anchor and refined into a pose-preserving, prior-compatible voxel support, enabling a frozen object generator to synthesize detailed textured geometry in the anchor-defined spatial reference. This coarse-to-fine design separates scene organization from object-scale detail synthesis, focusing scene-level training on spatial organization and anchor refinement while reusing pretrained object priors. Experiments demonstrate improvements in scene layout, object geometry, visual quality, and generalization. Bri3D predicts scene layouts efficiently; full scene generation is faster than SceneGen and SAM3D.

</details>


### [61] [Reviving the Discarded High-Resolution Feature for Transformer-Based Tiny Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07994)
*Zehao Wang,Si Wu,Guiping Cao,Xin Li,Yaowei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReDHF 以适度计算成本重新利用被丢弃的高分辨率特征，提升 DETR 类模型的微小目标检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微小目标像素少，细节容易在下采样中丢失；最高分辨率特征虽保留细节，但计算成本高，直接引入又会带来密集背景噪声。

Method: 框架包含三个协同模块：CSDF 跨尺度融合语义并将细节注入浅层特征；EGSS 利用几何自适应辅助监督学习分类与定位先验；FSQI 使解码器查询同时访问低层细节和高层语义。

Result: 最佳 ReDHF 变体相对各自基线在 AI-TOD-v2、VisDrone 和 SODA-D 上分别提升 1.4、1.6 和 1.5 AP；微小和小目标的收益尤其明显。

Conclusion: 在多个 DETR 变体和评估基准上，合理融合并监督高分辨率特征可持续提升检测性能；论文报告在所评估基准上达到最先进水平。

Abstract: Tiny object detection remains challenging because tiny instances occupy only a few pixels, and their fine-grained details are easily lost during feature downsampling. Although the highest-resolution feature preserves rich local details for tiny objects, DETR-like detectors often discard it due to its large computational cost, while directly reintroducing this feature also brings dense background noise. To address this problem, we propose ReDHF, a novel framework that Revives the Discarded High-resolution Feature for tiny object detection. ReDHF has three cooperative components. Cross-Scale Deformable Fusion (CSDF) first constructs a semantically enhanced highest-resolution feature and injects its fine-grained details into the shallow feature under a moderate cost. Ellipse-Guided Shallow Supervision (EGSS) then guides the enhanced feature to learn more reliable classification and localization priors by applying geometry-adaptive auxiliary supervision. Full-Scale Query Interaction (FSQI) further enables decoder queries to access both low-level details and high-level semantic information, allowing them to aggregate richer cues for tiny objects. Extensive experiments show that ReDHF consistently improves multiple baselines across different DETR variants and achieves state-of-the-art performance on the evaluated benchmarks. The best ReDHF variants surpass their baselines by +1.4 AP on AI-TOD-v2, +1.6 AP on VisDrone, and +1.5 AP on SODA-D, with especially notable gains on tiny and small objects.

</details>


### [62] [When GeoFMs Meet VHR: Benchmarking Foundation Models on Very High Resolution Earth Observation Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08003)
*Kshitij Raj Sharma,Pierre Adorni,Minh-Tan Pham,Dirk Tiede,Sébastien Lefèvre*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对超高分辨率遥感任务，大规模自然图像预训练和超高分辨率专用预训练均有优势；对强自然图像模型进行短期领域续训，可进一步提升表现，尤其是在标签稀缺时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地理空间基础模型主要使用地面采样距离为10米或更粗的Sentinel和Landsat影像预训练，其向能够分辨建筑、道路等细节的超高分辨率影像任务迁移的效果尚需系统评估。

Method: 构建VHR-Bench，包含10个数据集，覆盖5类任务、8个应用领域及卫星和航空平台，地面采样距离不超过1.5米。在冻结编码器条件下，以两种标注数据比例评估三个预训练层级的12个编码器；随后使用102万张留出的超高分辨率图像块，对DINOv3 ViT-L继续进行自监督预训练，并沿用相同下游评估方案。

Result: 标签稀缺时SkySense表现领先；使用全部标注数据时，网页图像预训练的DINOv3 ViT-L略微领先，两者表现接近。仅用Sentinel尺度影像预训练的编码器在RGB输入下迁移效果最差。续训后的DINOv3在标签稀缺时明显优于原始模型，在全部标注数据下也有小幅提升，且不增加推理参数量或计算量。

Conclusion: 结果支持“先泛化、后专门化”的策略：以大规模自然图像预训练模型为基础，辅以短期超高分辨率领域续训，可改善遥感任务迁移效果。摘要未提供具体数值，因而无法量化提升幅度或独立验证通用视觉特征的保持程度。

Abstract: Geospatial foundation models are a widely adopted starting point for remote sensing tasks. Most are pretrained on Sentinel-1/2 and Landsat imagery at a ground sampling distance (GSD) of 10m or coarser. Many applications instead rely on very high resolution (VHR) imagery, where individual buildings, roads, and amenities are resolved. We study which pretraining regimes transfer well to this scale. We introduce VHR-Bench, a benchmark suite of ten datasets at 1.5m GSD or finer spanning five task families, eight application domains, and both satellite and aerial platforms. We evaluate twelve encoders in three pretraining tiers with frozen backbones at two training-data fractions. We find that both pretraining on large natural-image collections and VHR-specific pretraining transfer effectively to VHR tasks, with complementary strengths. SkySense leads when labels are scarce; at the full label fraction the web-pretrained DINOv3 ViT-L edges ahead, with the two close together there. Encoders pretrained only on Sentinel-scale imagery transfer least well under RGB input. Guided by these complementary results, we continue the self-supervised pretraining of DINOv3 ViT-L on 1.02 million held-out VHR tiles and evaluate the resulting encoder without changing the downstream protocol. The continued model gives a clear improvement over the released backbone when labels are scarce and a modest one at the full fraction, at no increase in inference parameters or compute. These findings support a generalize-then-specialize strategy: already strong, broad natural-image pretraining can be further improved by a short VHR continuation stage while preserving its general visual features.

</details>


### [63] [Deformable Contact-Aware 3D Object Placement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08005)
*Songhe Wang,David Miller,Charles A. Kamhoua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DCAP将语言引导的3D物体放置建模为释放后达到物理平衡的过程，在自然度、穿透和稳定性指标上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法直接预测刚体6自由度位姿，忽略接触与软表面形变，容易导致悬浮、穿透或不自然的放置。

Method: 根据语义意图构造包含释放位置、物体尺度和可变形部件的释放状态，再通过刚体—可变形体耦合仿真，在重力和摩擦作用下达到平衡。

Result: 在2,063个提示词—资产—场景案例上，相比最强原始刚体基线，标准放置的VLM物理与自然度评分提高21.2点、人工偏好提高26.5点，平均穿透深度降低72.3%；相比仿真展开基线，残余速度降低72.1%。

Conclusion: 结果支持将语义意图与物理实现分开建模的有效性；但摘要中的实验不能证明物理准确放置必然要求二者严格分离。

Abstract: Current language-guided 3D object placement typically predicts a rigid 6-DoF pose, ignoring the physical reality that indoor placements are dictated by contact. Consequently, predicted poses often float, interpenetrate supports, or fail to deform soft surfaces. We propose Deformable Contact-Aware Placement (DCAP), a framework that reframes placement as a drop-to-equilibrium process. Rather than predicting a static pose, DCAP constructs a semantic release state that specifies the drop location, object scale, and deformable components, allowing a coupled rigid-deformable simulator to settle the object under gravity and friction. This explicitly separates semantic intent from physical realization. Evaluated on a benchmark of 2,063 prompt-asset-scene cases, DCAP significantly outperforms static baselines. On standard placements, it improves VLM Physics & Naturalness by 21.2 points and human preference by 26.5 points over the strongest raw rigid baseline, while reducing mean penetration depth by 72.3%. Compared to simulated rollout baselines, DCAP reduces residual speed by 72.1%. Ultimately, our results prove that physically accurate 3D placement requires modeling semantic intent and physical realization as strictly separate processes.

</details>


### [64] [NutriVisRAG: Multimodal Exemplar Grounding for Nutrient Estimation with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08054)
*Lubnaa Abdur Rahman,Ioannis Papathanail,Maria Panagiotou,Nefeli Dimitropoulou,Margret Harðardóttir,Elisabeth J. den Brok,Bastiaan E. de Galan,Stavroula Georgia Mougiakakou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NutriVisRAG通过检索具有营养标注的相似餐食图像，作为多模态示例辅助视觉语言模型估计营养；多数但并非所有配置获得改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然生活场景下，仅凭餐食图像准确评估膳食营养仍具挑战；现有检索方法主要依赖文本食物表示，缺少对视觉相似且带营养标注餐食的直接利用。

Method: 使用视觉编码器检索相似餐食及其营养标注，将图像和标注作为推理时的多模态上下文示例，无需任务专属微调。评估覆盖4个数据集、4个视觉语言模型、3种检索编码器和2种提示策略。

Result: 96种配置中有56种降低主要估计误差；整餐提示为35/48，逐项提示为21/48。16个模型—数据集组合中，13个的最佳NutriVisRAG误差低于最佳Vanilla基线。GPT-5.4-mini采用整餐提示及各数据集最佳检索编码器时，四个数据集的主要误差降低1.7%—35.7%。

Conclusion: 在推理阶段引入带标注的相似餐食图像，可在无需任务专属微调的情况下改善营养估计，但效果依赖模型、数据集、检索编码器和提示策略等配置。

Abstract: Diet is a major determinant of health, yet accurate dietary assessment from free-living meal images remains challenging. Existing retrieval-based Vision-Language Model (VLM) approaches to nutrient estimation typically rely on textual food representations, while, to our knowledge, direct grounding on visually similar annotated meals has not been investigated. We present NutriVisRAG, a retrieval-augmented generation framework that uses a vision encoder to retrieve similar meals with known nutritional annotations and provide them as multimodal in-context exemplars for VLM nutrient estimation at inference time, without task-specific fine-tuning. We evaluate NutriVisRAG across four datasets, four VLMs, three retrieval encoders, and two prompting strategies, including free-living meals from adults with insulin-treated diabetes, where carbohydrate estimates are compared against two dietitians' annotations. Across all 96 configurations (4 datasets x 4 VLMs x 3 retrieval encoders x 2 prompting strategies), NutriVisRAG reduced the primary estimation error in 56, with improvements more frequent under whole-meal (35/48) than per-item prompting (21/48). Across the evaluated configurations, the lowest NutriVisRAG error was below the best Vanilla error in 13 of 16 VLM--dataset pairs. For GPT-5.4-mini, under whole-meal prompting, the best-performing retrieval encoder for each dataset reduced the primary error on all four datasets relative to Vanilla, with reductions of 1.7--35.7%. Overall, our results show that grounding in multimodal exemplars comprising images and respective annotations can improve nutrient estimation at inference time without task-specific fine-tuning, although the benefits remain configuration-dependent.

</details>


### [65] [Spatiotemporal Dynamics-Aware Industrial Anomaly Detection and Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08080)
*S-Wei Chen,Siangyu Yang,Yi-Chen Jan,Chun-Yi Lee,Shao-Yuan Lo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MUSTARD 利用光流引导的关键帧选择和多模态增强，使多模态大语言模型无需参数更新即可检测并解释工业动态异常。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工业异常检测主要依赖静态视觉特征，难以识别运动异常；现有多模态大语言模型的时序建模能力也存在不足。

Method: 通过光流引导选择关键帧，并结合增强模态表征局部运动模式和全局动态演化；以少量正常参考样本为基础进行推理，无需更新模型。

Result: 摘要称，该方法在时空异常检测和生成合理解释方面表现优异，但未提供具体数据或基线。

Conclusion: MUSTARD 为动态工业异常检测提供了可跨产品高效适配、具有决策可解释性的方案；作者称其为首个基于多模态大语言模型的此类框架。

Abstract: Industrial Anomaly Detection (IAD) is a long-established problem that aims to detect defects in industrial products during manufacturing for quality control. Most existing studies focus on static visual patterns as the basis for detection. However, in real manufacturing environments, many anomalies are dynamics-based, such as irregular movements that cannot be perceived from static cues alone. We observe that even recent Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM)-based approaches fall short in identifying such dynamic anomalies due to limited temporal modeling capability. To address this, we introduce MUSTARD: MUltimodal SpatioTemporal Anomaly Reasoning and Detection, a framework that equips MLLMs with fine-grained spatiotemporal dynamics awareness for detecting and reasoning about dynamics-based defects. MUSTARD introduces optical-flow-guided keyframe selection and augmented modalities to capture critical motion irregularities through local motion patterns and global dynamic evolution. Its normal pattern-based reasoning pipeline requires only a few normal reference samples as the detection basis, avoiding any model updates. This enables efficient adaptation across various products and provides high decision interpretability. MUSTARD is the first MLLM-based framework for dynamics-based IAD. Experiments demonstrate its superior performance in detecting spatiotemporal anomalies and providing plausible explanations. The code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [66] [TerraMesh-Masks: Open-Vocabulary Segmentation for Earth Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08081)
*Benedikt Blumenstiel,Hugues Devimeux,Johannes Jakubik,Konrad Schindler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出地球观测开放词汇分割数据集 TerraMesh-Masks，并构建开放词汇版 TerraMind，取得 35.18% IoU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用开放词汇分割模型因语义、尺度、传感模态和地理背景差异，难以迁移到地球观测领域；该领域也缺少大规模专用数据集与基准。

Method: 构建含 1600 万个二值掩码的数据集，对齐 900 万个多模态 TerraMesh 样本，使用 OpenStreetMap 和 Overture 衍生语义标注；另设经专家审核的评估集，并构建开放词汇版 TerraMind。

Result: 专家审核评估集包含 523 个样本、覆盖 200 多个类别。所构建模型达到 35.18% IoU，高于 DINOv3.txt 的 16.29% 和 SAM-3 的 11.33%。

Conclusion: 地球观测领域专用数据与模型有助于提升开放词汇分割性能；数据集已采用宽松许可证公开发布。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary segmentation (OVS) enables models to segment a variety of concepts specified in natural language, removing the need for fixed label sets and fine-tuning. While recent OVS approaches have shown strong performance on natural images, they transfer poorly to Earth observation (EO) data due to differing semantics, scales, sensing modality, and geographic context. To date, large-scale EO-specific datasets and benchmarks for OVS remain scarce. We, therefore, introduce TerraMesh-Masks, a large-scale open-vocabulary segmentation dataset for EO, comprising 16 million binary masks aligned with nine million multimodal TerraMesh samples and annotated using OpenStreetMap and Overture-derived semantics. In addition, we provide an expert-reviewed evaluation set of 523 samples covering over 200 classes to enable robust evaluation. To demonstrate the impact of TerraMesh-Masks, we build an open-vocabulary version of TerraMind. Our results demonstrate significantly improved performance over general-purpose state-of-the-art models reaching 35.18% IoU versus 16.29% for DINOv3.txt and 11.33% for SAM-3. We release the dataset publicly under a permissive license at https://huggingface.co/datasets/ibm-esa-geospatial/TerraMesh-Masks.

</details>


### [67] [National-scale mapping of tree species composition in Switzerland from high-resolution aerial imagery and multimodal data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08095)
*Yixin Zhou,Luca Ferrari,Verena C. Griess,Nataliia Rehush,Konrad Schindler,Mirela Beloiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究利用多源遥感和深度学习，绘制瑞士30米分辨率的树种混合比例地图，补充传统优势树种地图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统森林地图通常仅标示每个像元的优势树种，遗漏伴生树种比例，难以充分支持森林生产力评估和气候扰动响应研究。

Method: 以瑞士国家森林清查的3,992个样地胸高断面积观测为训练数据，融合0.1米航空RGB影像、机载激光扫描、年度AlphaEarth卫星嵌入及气候变量，利用深度学习估计10种主要树种及其他类的胸高断面积占比。

Result: 空间交叉验证的11类宏平均R²为0.485；独立森林保护区数据上，像元和样地尺度宏平均R²分别为0.552和0.733，优势类别识别准确率分别为73.2%和81.1%。地图揭示了广泛分布但占比较小的槭树和白蜡树，并再现了随海拔变化的树种序列。

Conclusion: 像元内树种混合比例地图可为空间连续的森林监测、混交林经营及气候适应规划提供支持，并补充实地清查统计；摘要未报告更广泛区域的模型泛化能力。

Abstract: Tree-species composition shapes forest productivity and how forests respond to climate disturbances such as drought, windthrow and insect outbreaks. Yet most national forest maps assign each pixel a single dominant species and cannot show the share of accompanying species. We mapped the basal-area shares of ten main tree species and a residual Other class across Switzerland at 30 m resolution. A deep-learning model combining 0.1 m aerial RGB imagery, airborne laser scanning (ALS), annual AlphaEarth satellite embeddings, and climate variables was trained on basal-area observations from 3,992 Swiss National Forest Inventory (NFI) sites. In spatial cross-validation, the model reached a macro-$R^2$ of 0.485 across the 11 classes. On an independent forest-reserve dataset, the map reached a macro-$R^2$ of 0.552 for individual 30 m pixels and 0.733 for plots, and identified the dominant class in 73.2% of pixels and 81.1% of plots. The map shows maple and ash as widespread minor components that dominant-class maps omit. It also reproduces the elevational sequence from broadleaved lowland stands through spruce to larch and Arolla pine near the treeline. By resolving the species mixture within each 30 m pixel, the map complements field inventory statistics with spatially continuous information that can support forest monitoring, the management of mixed stands, and climate-adaptation planning across Switzerland.

</details>


### [68] [Towards Practical 20 kbps Generative Video Compression via Adaptive Tokenization and Entropy Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08099)
*Jiancheng Zhao,Jixiang Luo,Haibin Huang,Chi Zhang,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将预训练视频生成表征转化为面向压缩的离散表征，并联合优化分词器与熵模型，提升超低码率视频压缩的感知质量和编码效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超低码率下，有限的比特预算容易导致感知重要细节丢失；现有方法多在重建阶段利用生成先验，且生成与重建表征未必适合熵编码。

Method: 将 MiniMax-H3 的预训练因果视频 VAE 适配为离散视频分词器；构建捕捉局部与全局 token 依赖的小型自回归熵模型，采用分组预测实现并行解码；通过可微码率代理联合优化分词器和熵模型，在保持重建质量的同时降低编码成本。

Result: 在 UVG、MCL-JCV 和 HEVC Class B 数据集上，相较 GLC-Video，BD-LPIPS 节省 82.2%–87.5%，BD-DISTS 节省 78.3%–85.0%，表现出更优的感知率失真性能。

Conclusion: 将预训练视频表征直接适配为压缩表征，并利用码率梯度联合优化，有助于改善超低码率下的感知压缩性能；摘要未提供计算开销等细节。

Abstract: Video compression at ultra-low bitrates remains challenging due to the loss of perceptually important details under limited bit budgets. Recent generative video compression methods leverage pretrained video priors to improve perceptual quality and temporal coherence, but many follow a compress-then-generate paradigm, where generative priors are primarily exploited during reconstruction. Moreover, representations learned for generation and reconstruction are not inherently optimized for entropy coding.
  In this work, we propose \method, which adapts pretrained video representations into compression-oriented representations while preserving their reconstruction capability. Specifically, we adapt the pretrained causal video VAE of MiniMax-H3 into a discrete video tokenizer and develop a small autoregressive entropy model that captures local and global token dependencies, with grouped prediction for parallel decoding. To further improve coding efficiency, we jointly optimize the tokenizer and entropy model using a differentiable rate surrogate, allowing rate gradients to adapt token assignments toward lower coding costs while preserving reconstruction quality. Experiments on UVG, MCL-JCV, and HEVC Class B demonstrate superior perceptual rate--distortion performance, achieving BD-LPIPS savings of 82.2\%--87.5\% and BD-DISTS savings of 78.3\%--85.0\% relative to GLC-Video.

</details>


### [69] [ProtoGestalt: Interpretable Prototypes for Facial Phenotyping and Diagnosis of Rare Genetic Disorders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08125)
*Ranga Baminiwatte,Alexander Hustinx,Fabio Hellmann,Aaron J Masino*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ProtoGestalt 是一种基于原型的可解释面部表型分析框架，用于罕见病筛查及综合征识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 罕见遗传病种类繁多、临床特征重叠，诊断困难；现有面部诊断模型的可解释性有限，也并非主要针对未确诊疾病的广泛筛查而设计。

Method: 扩展原型学习，将学习到的视觉特征约束在选定面部区域，以局部、可加的证据解释预测；在多个对照数据集和不同类别比例下评估筛查能力，并测试训练期间见过的综合征识别能力。

Result: 原型表征能够显著区分综合征患者与病例匹配对照的面部图像；对照比例增加时仍保持一定效能，并能区分不同综合征。摘要未报告具体性能指标。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了与具有解剖意义的面部区域相联系的可解释预测，可能辅助临床决策和表型—基因关联发现；其实际临床价值仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Rare genetic disorders are difficult to diagnose due to their diversity and overlapping clinical features. Facial morphology can provide diagnostic clues, yet most diagnostic models offer limited interpretability and are not designed to support broad screening for undiagnosed disease. We present ProtoGestalt, an interpretable prototype-based framework for facial phenotyping, rare-disease screening, and syndrome identification. The method extends prototype learning by constraining learned visual features to select facial regions, allowing predictions to be explained through localized and additive evidence. We assess ProtoGestalt's ability to distinguish syndromic from unaffected faces on multiple control datasets and class prevalence values. We further evaluate it's diagnostic performance on syndromes observed during training. The learned prototype-based representations provide significant separation between syndromic and case-matched control images and retain useful performance as the proportion of controls increases. The framework also demonstrates the ability to differentiate among syndromes. Although reasonably accurate specialized models for facial classification of rare disorders already exist, ProtoGestalt offers an interpretable alternative that links predictions to anatomically meaningful facial regions, potentially supporting clinical decision-making and the discovery of phenotype-gene associations.

</details>


### [70] [FreeOcc: Decoupling Ego-Motion for Efficient 4D Occupancy Forecasting via Continuous Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08128)
*Zeping Zhang,Zhuoya Zhao,Samy Metari,Robert Laganière*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FreeOcc 利用显式自车运动解耦和不确定性感知计算，实现轻量、高效且鲁棒的四维占用预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有四维占用世界模型依赖计算开销较大的交叉注意力或深度递归来区分自车运动与场景动态，却未充分利用运动规划器提供的自车未来运动。

Method: 将显式 SE(2) 几何解耦引入条件流匹配，构建动态特征空间；采用无注意力时间融合，并用物理约束常微分方程建模剩余运动。通过不确定性感知混合，将观测充分的体素交给单次解码器，仅对高不确定性区域使用 ODE 求解器。

Result: 模型有 28.3M 参数，约为同类模型的 40%；集成模式达到 27 FPS。在 nuScenes 上取得 0.47 m 近场倒角距离和 32.1% mIoU；可零样本迁移至 SemanticKITTI，并在严重传感器丢包和时间戳抖动下保留理想 mIoU 的 92.8%。

Conclusion: 显式几何解耦结合按不确定性分配计算，可兼顾预测精度、推理效率和鲁棒性；上述表现与领先性为摘要报告，具体对比设置及迁移指标未给出。

Abstract: Current 4D occupancy world models use heavy cross-attention or deep recurrence to disentangle ego-motion from scene dynamics. This requires significant computational capacity. However, this effort is largely redundant since the ego-vehicle's intended future motion is provided by the downstream motion planner. We present FreeOcc, a lightweight continuous-time world model that introduces explicit $SE(2)$ geometric decoupling into a conditional flow-matching framework. By mathematically factoring out self-motion, we create a purely dynamic feature space. This space supports attention-free temporal fusion and allows physics-constrained ODEs to operate strictly on residual agent kinematics. To maximize deployment efficiency, FreeOcc introduces an uncertainty-aware blending mechanism. This routes well-observed voxels to a single-pass decoder, reserving the ODE solver exclusively for ambiguous, high-uncertainty regions. Requiring only 28.3M parameters-roughly 40% of comparable models-our optimal ensemble mode (FreeOcc-E) runs at 27 FPS. On nuScenes, FreeOcc achieves state-of-the-art forecasting performance with a 0.47 m Near Field Chamfer Distance (NFCD) and 32.1% mIoU. Furthermore, the model transfers zero-shot to SemanticKITTI. It also proves highly robust, retaining 92.8% of its ideal mIoU under significant sensor packet loss and timestamp jitter.

</details>


### [71] [Describe Your Facial Expressions by Linking Image Encoders and Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08138)
*Yujian Yuan,Jiabei Zeng,Shiguang Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出面部表情描述任务，利用合成图文数据和三阶段训练生成细粒度表情文本，并提升相关视觉识别任务的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统情绪类别和面部动作单元（AU）难以充分表达面部动作及情绪状态的细微差异，且缺少表情描述所需的图文配对数据。

Method: 利用GPT-3.5结合现有情绪类别或AU标注，合成并组合描述文本，构建图文训练对；采用BLIP-2预训练技术，通过三阶段框架训练视觉语言模型。

Result: 模型能够描述面部动作、情绪及两者的推断关系，并能描述训练数据中未出现的情绪；中间视觉特征在AU检测和情绪分类任务上的表现也得到提升。

Conclusion: 合成标注与视觉语言训练可缓解表情描述数据不足，支持细粒度描述，并改善相关视觉表征；摘要未提供具体量化结果。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel task of describing human facial expressions of a facial image in natural language, which captures the nuances of facial actions and emotional states beyond traditional emotion categories or facial action units (AUs). To achieve the facial expression captioning model, we propose a three-stage training framework that trains a vision-to-language model using synthesized image-text pairs and the BLIP-2 pre-training techniques. To overcome the challenge of missing training image-text pairs for facial expression captioning, we propose a strategy that involves synthesizing and combining captions using GPT-3.5 and existing annotations on either emotion categories or AUs. Experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our method in generating captions that describe details of facial actions and emotions, as well as the inferential relationship between them, even when those emotions are not present in the training data. It is also demonstrated that the vision-to-language task enhances the performance of the intermediate visual features on both AU detection and emotion classification tasks.

</details>


### [72] [Fault-Guided Cross-Layer Prediction for Unsupervised Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08140)
*Tao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 通过预测冻结视觉Transformer不同层之间的特征转移，利用不可传递的信息检测工业异常，并结合训练集校准的前景先验提升定位效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单类别无监督工业异常检测只能使用单一产品类别的正常图像训练；深层特征虽保留语义上下文，却可能丢失局部细节，需要利用不同深度间的信息差异识别缺陷。

Method: 将冻结视觉Transformer的深度视为离散状态转移，训练有向预测器逐token预测层间隐藏状态；通过相邻层余弦相似度识别特征变化断层并确定源层和目标层。结合仅用训练图像校准的有界前景先验，抑制背景证据，同时保留前景估计区域之外的缺陷。

Result: 图像级AUROC：MVTec-AD 99.75%、VisA 99.42%、Real-IAD 94.27%；Real-IAD对象级AUROC为96.26%。在256×256分辨率下，三者像素级P-AUROC/P-AUPRO分别为98.46%/95.97%、99.21%/96.12%、99.26%/96.59%。

Conclusion: 层间特征转移及其断层结构可用于单类别工业异常检测；源层和目标层的选取规则可迁移至ViT-B和RADIO-B，并在三个数据集上取得较高检测与定位指标。

Abstract: Feature reconstruction is widely used for unsupervised industrial anomaly detection in the single-class setting, where only normal images from one product category are available for training. We propose a single-class detector that asks what information one block can pass to another. We treat the depth of a frozen vision transformer as a sequence of discrete transitions. A directed predictor learns a block-to-block hidden-state transition pointwise over tokens. Adjacent-layer cosine similarity exposes four sharp fault lines in DINOv2 ViT-L, separating stable depth segments. The deepest blocks retain semantic context but lose local detail; a prediction from deep or shallow sources to sensitive targets therefore exposes the information that cannot be transported. The fault structure fixes the source and target roles, and the same rule transfers to ViT-B and RADIO-B. We use a bounded foreground prior calibrated solely on training images: it attenuates background evidence without also erasing defects outside the estimated foreground. Using one predictor architecture and a common training-only calibration protocol, image AUROC is 99.75 on MVTec-AD, 99.42 on VisA and 94.27 on Real-IAD. On Real-IAD the object-level AUROC is 96.26. At 256x256 pixel resolution, P-AUROC/P-AUPRO are 98.46/95.97 on MVTec-AD, 99.21/96.12 on VisA and 99.26/96.59 on Real-IAD.

</details>


### [73] [MCUFlowNet: Hardware-Aware Neural Architecture Search for Optical Flow on Microcontrollers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08147)
*Enmin Lin,Jona Beysens*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PACE-NAS通过宏观架构固定峰值SRAM、微观搜索优化精度与延迟，得到适用于MCU的光流模型MCUFlowNet。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密光流有助于小型无人机避障，但带神经加速器的微控制器受片上SRAM限制，可处理的输入分辨率受限，且专用网络较少。

Method: 宏观设计确定骨干网络及峰值SRAM；微观设计搜索算子和网络深度，以编译器估计延迟权衡精度。针对EdgeFlowNet，将输出头中的转置卷积替换为双线性上采样与卷积，并引入两个轻量级上下文模块。

Result: 峰值SRAM降低18%，上下文模块在不增加峰值的情况下降低端点误差。MCUFlowNet-S比重新训练的EdgeFlowNet快1.76倍，EPE高1.6%；在2米/秒模拟森林飞行中，无碰撞到达率为51.5%，对照为41.8%。语义分割中，搜索得到的PIDNet-S变体在精度相近时快1.17倍。

Conclusion: 将内存约束与延迟搜索解耦，可有效探索适合MCU的精度—速度权衡；该方法也可用于语义分割。

Abstract: Dense optical flow allows small drones to avoid obstacles. However, few dense optical flow networks are designed for microcontrollers (MCUs) with neural accelerators, whose on-chip SRAM limits the input resolution. We present PACE-NAS, a macro-to-micro neural architecture search framework that decouples memory from latency: a macro design fixes the backbone and its peak SRAM, and a micro design searches operators and depths to trade accuracy against compiler-estimated latency. We apply it to EdgeFlowNet on a Grove Vision AI V2 board (Ethos-U55), where profiling locates the peak SRAM at the full-resolution output head. Replacing transposed convolutions with bilinear upsampling and convolutions lowers the peak SRAM by 18%, and two lightweight context modules lower the endpoint error (EPE) without raising the peak. All searched subnets share this peak, so the search needs no memory constraint. We name the Pareto-optimal models MCUFlowNet; the fastest variant, MCUFlowNet-S, runs 1.76x faster than a retrained EdgeFlowNet at a 1.6% higher EPE and, in simulated forest flights at 2 m/s, reaches the goal without collision in 51.5% of trials, against 41.8%. PACE-NAS also applies to semantic segmentation, where a searched PIDNet-S variant runs 1.17x faster at similar accuracy. Code is available at https://gitlab.kuleuven.be/emedia-research-lab/esat-stadius/mcuflownet/mcuflownet.

</details>


### [74] [Improving Context-Shift Robustness of Convolutional Networks via Context-Regularized Cross-Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08157)
*Jinen Setpal,Chaoyue Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文利用更忠实的空间特征归因与上下文正则化，降低图像分类模型对背景的依赖，提升上下文变化下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试场景中的对象上下文可能与训练数据不同；模型若依赖非因果的背景线索，性能便容易下降。同时，现有特征归因方法存在理论局限。

Method: 提出 ContrastiveCAMs，生成更忠实反映模型预测的空间归因图；在此基础上设计上下文正则化交叉熵损失（CR-CE），抑制上下文区域的影响，引导模型关注对象相关特征。

Result: 在 Waterbirds、Spawrious、ImageNet/ImageNet-BG 和 Hard-ImageNet 等数据集上，上下文偏移鲁棒性均得到改善；摘要未提供具体数值。

Conclusion: 将空间归因用于训练正则化，有助于减少卷积神经网络对上下文线索的依赖，并增强其应对上下文偏移的能力。

Abstract: Image classification models often encounter objects in diverse and unpredictable contexts at test time, which may differ substantially from those seen during training. This phenomenon, termed context shift, exposes a key fragility in neural networks: when models rely on non-causal contextual cues for prediction, shifts in context can lead to significant performance degradation. In this paper, we propose a novel training framework to improve the robustness of convolutional neural networks under context shift. Our approach leverages spatial feature attribution to guide models toward making predictions that rely less on contextual regions and more on object-relevant features. As a first step, recognizing a theoretical limitation of existing feature attribution methods we introduce a new variant, ContrastiveCAMs, which produces more faithful attribution maps of model predictions.
