Building on ContrastiveCAMs, we further propose Context-Regularized Cross-Entropy (CR-CE), a modification of the standard cross-entropy loss that regularizes the model's attention by suppressing the influence of contextual regions, thereby improving robustness to context shift. We evaluate the effectiveness of our approach on several medium-to-large scale datasets (Waterbirds, Spawrious, ImageNet/ImageNet-BG, Hard-ImageNet), and report consistent improvements in context-shift robustness.

</details>


### [75] [Multimodal Hierarchical Mixture-of-Experts for 3D Medical Vision-Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08185)
*Gizem Mert,Szymon Płotka,Pedro R. A. S. Bassi,Wenxuan Li,Zongwei Zhou,Jarosław B. Ćwikła,Arkadiusz Sitek,Ewa Szczurek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MedHiVE 利用两级软混合专家路由与解剖级、全局对比学习，提升三维医学影像的多模态表征能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学视觉语言模型往往忽略影像和报告的解剖层级，难以聚焦具有临床意义的局部区域。

Method: 将图像和文本 token 依次通过局部与全局 SMoE 进行可学习路由和融合，并在解剖区域及整体扫描两个层级开展视觉—语言对比对齐。

Result: 在超过 2.5 万例 CT 扫描上预训练；在 6 个数据集的分割、分类和检测任务中，表现持续优于 11 种先进方法。摘要未提供具体指标。

Conclusion: 解剖层级建模及跨模态动态信息流有助于提升三维医学影像任务表现；具体提升幅度和泛化边界仍需查阅全文。

Abstract: Medical 3D scans and reports are inherently hierarchical, with diagnostic cues concentrated in specific anatomical regions across multiple levels of organization. Yet, most medical vision-language models ignore this structure and fail to focus on clinically relevant anatomy. We introduce MedHiVE, which combines hierarchical vision-language Soft Mixture-of-Experts (SMoE) with contrastive learning to jointly model fine-grained anatomy and global scan context. To our knowledge, MedHiVE is the first framework to route both vision and text tokens through a two-level SMoE hierarchy organized by anatomical scale, coupling this with report-derived anatomy-level contrastive alignment for 3D medical imaging. This replaces the direct region-text alignment of statically pooled features and vision-only hierarchical routing of prior work with dynamic, slot-based cross-modal information flow across anatomical scales. MedHiVE operates via a two-level, learnable token-routing mechanism, where local image and text features are first routed through a local SMoE and then fused by a global SMoE into unified multimodal representations. Contrastive learning aligns both anatomy-level and global vision-language representations in a unified space. Pre-trained on 25,000+ CT scans with anatomical supervision, MedHiVE consistently surpasses 11 state-of-the-art methods across 6 datasets on 3 tasks: segmentation, classification, and detection, demonstrating the importance of hierarchical modeling. The code for MedHiVE is at github.com/szczurek-lab/MedHiVE.

</details>


### [76] [SplatAnything: Gaussian Spacing Normalization for Feed-forward 3DGS across Arbitrary Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08264)
*Hao Yu,Zhenwei Wang,Yongzhen Hu,Yuning Gong,Junta Wu,Xiaowei Zhou,Hujun Bao,Tengfei Wang,Chunchao Guo,Sida Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SplatAnything通过高斯间距归一化，缓解前馈式3D高斯泼溅中尺度监督不一致的问题，并在多样场景和视角下取得领先表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常按场景径向距离归一化，但相邻高斯中心的间距仍随场景尺度和相机配置显著变化，导致高斯尺度预测目标发生系统性偏移，形成模糊监督。

Method: 提出高斯间距归一化（GSNorm），对场景级高斯间距的稳健统计量进行规范化，为高斯属性预测提供更一致的空间参考；同时建立覆盖多数据集、不同输入稀疏程度和外推视角的基准。

Result: 摘要报告该方法达到当前最先进性能，在场景尺度和视角变化较大时提升尤为明显；未提供具体数值。

Conclusion: 以高斯间距而非仅依赖径向统计量建立空间参考，有助于改善监督一致性和跨场景、跨视角的表现。

Abstract: We identify a key limitation of existing feed-forward 3DGS methods: Gaussian attributes are typically predicted in a radial-distance-normalized space that canonicalizes a global radial statistic of the reconstructed geometry, while the spacing between neighboring Gaussian centers can still vary substantially across scene scales and camera configurations. This mismatch induces systematic shifts in Gaussian-scale targets, leading to ambiguous supervision. To address this, we propose SplatAnything with Gaussian Spacing Normalization (GSNorm), which canonicalizes a robust scene-level statistic of Gaussian spacing to provide a more consistent spatial reference for Gaussian prediction. To rigorously assess our method, we further establish a systematic benchmark spanning diverse datasets, input sparsity levels, and challenging extrapolated viewpoints. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SplatAnything achieves state-of-the-art performance, with particularly strong gains under substantial variations in scene scale and viewpoint.

</details>


### [77] [DART: Domain-Agnostic Residual Transfer for Generalist Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08271)
*Muhammad Aqeel,Maham Nazir,Marco Cristani,Francesco Setti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DART通过解耦异常残差中的异常线索与域特定因素，提高少样本通用异常检测的跨域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有正常—异常引导方法的残差混杂了可迁移的异常信息与纹理、几何及采集条件等域特定因素，导致新域上的迁移受限和虚假激活。

Method: 提出架构无关的即插即用框架：残差解耦模块保留异常判别信息并抑制域信息；跨域元训练通过构建跨源域任务促进有效解耦。该框架可插入残差计算与检测头之间。

Result: 在MVTec AD、VisA和BraTS上，相较多个基础方法均取得一致改进，且域偏移越强时收益通常越大；摘要未提供具体数值。

Conclusion: 将残差解耦与跨域元训练结合，是增强正常—异常引导型通用异常检测跨域迁移能力的有效、可插拔方案。

Abstract: Generalist Anomaly Detection (GAD) aims to detect anomalies in unseen domains using only a few reference samples. Recent normal-abnormal guided methods improve this setting by exploiting abnormal references, but their residuals often mix transferable anomaly cues with domain-specific appearance factors such as texture, geometry, and acquisition conditions. This residual domain entanglement limits cross-domain transfer and may produce spurious activations on novel domains. We propose DART, a plug-in framework for normal-abnormal guided GAD that learns to separate residuals into anomaly-relevant and domain-specific components. DART combines a Residual Disentanglement Module, which preserves anomaly-discriminative information while suppressing domain information, with Cross-Domain Meta-Training, which forms episodes across source domains to make the separation effective. The method is architecture-agnostic and can be inserted between residual computation and the detection head of existing GAD pipelines. Experiments on MVTec AD, VisA, and BraTS show consistent improvements over multiple base methods, with the largest gains under stronger domain shifts. https://github.com/aqeeelmirza/DART

</details>


### [78] [Interactive Visual Gym: Evaluating Visual Interaction Models with Simulated Users in Interactive Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08286)
*Chenhao Zheng,Zixian Ma,Kai Li,Kanchana Ranasinghe,Jieyu Zhang,Artemis Panagopoulou,Weikai Huang,Jianxu Shangguan,Yuanyuan Tan,Silvio Savarese,Juan Carlos Niebles,Ranjay Krishna,Ran Xu,Honglu Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出闭环视觉交互评测与训练框架 IVG，并构建包含315个场景的 IVG-Bench；现有模型表现仍有限，闭环训练则显示出改进潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线回放基准无法反映助手回答对用户后续行为和任务结果的影响，因此需要闭环评估。

Method: 联合模拟可执行任务环境与用户，构建烹饪、家居操作和移动界面三个模拟器，以及受用户画像条件控制、包含执行错误和偏离计划行为的用户模拟机制；通过人类研究验证评估协议，并开展 GRPO 概念验证实验。

Result: 最佳模型 Gemini 4 Argon 在限时内完成53.4%的任务；模型在事实依据、可执行指导和响应延迟方面存在明显不足。交互质量指标与参与者偏好的一致率为78.5%；闭环训练提升了 Qwen3.5-4B 在烹饪环境中的任务成功率和交互质量。

Conclusion: 闭环模拟有助于评估视觉助手对动态任务的实际影响，并能提供训练信号；但现有模型的可靠性与实时性仍有待提高。

Abstract: Visual interaction models that support users during ongoing physical tasks require evaluation in closed loop, where assistant responses can change subsequent user behavior and task outcomes. Existing benchmarks largely rely on offline replay and therefore cannot capture these effects. We introduce Interactive Visual Gym, a framework for closed-loop evaluation and training of visual interaction models through joint simulation of executable task environments and human users. We contribute three simulators that recreate everyday tasks and human-aligned action spaces across cooking, household manipulation, and mobile-interface domains, together with a persona-conditioned user simulation harness that collaborates with the assistant while incorporating controlled execution errors and other out-of-plan behaviors. We instantiate the framework as IVG-Bench, a benchmark with 315 interaction scenarios. Evaluating state-of-the-art VLMs as visual interaction models reveals substantial limitations: the best-performing model, Gemini 4 Argon, completes only 53.4% of tasks within the time limit, exhibits significant weaknesses in factual grounding and actionable guidance, and is often too slow to prevent errors when response latency is considered. We verify our evaluation protocol with a human study which finds 78.5% agreement between our interaction-quality metric and participant preferences. We further show that the environments provide useful learning signals: in a proof-of-concept GRPO experiment, closed-loop training improves both task success and interaction quality for Qwen3.5-4B model in our cooking environment.

</details>


### [79] [ReferClaw: Disambiguating User Intent for Egocentric Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07683)
*Hangyu Qin,Junbin Xiao,Angela Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReferClaw 通过多智能体主动澄清用户意图，提升第一人称视频问答的答案准确性和视觉依据匹配表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一人称助手面对简短、含糊的问题时，视频和对话历史可能不足以唯一确定指代对象；直接选定一种解释容易产生与用户意图不符的答案。

Method: 将流式视频问答建模为交互过程，由三个协作智能体识别候选意图、提出澄清问题，并依据确认的意图及视觉证据作答；采用考虑成本和轮次限制的策略，权衡信息增益与用户负担，并通过意图特定技能更新、过程奖励引导的策略优化及智能体级信用分配进行学习。

Result: 在增强的第一人称问答基准上，相比现有基线提高了答案准确率及答案与视觉依据的联合表现，并展现出跨数据集零样本迁移能力；摘要未提供具体数值。

Conclusion: 交互式澄清是解决第一人称流式辅助场景中在线用户意图歧义的可行方法。

Abstract: Users of egocentric AI assistants often ask short, ambiguous questions whose intended referents cannot be uniquely resolved from video and dialogue history. For example, "Where did I leave it?" may refer to several previously handled objects. Existing VideoQA systems typically commit to a single interpretation, risking plausible but intention-misaligned answers. We introduce ReferClaw, an agentic framework that formulates egocentric streaming QA as an interactive process: the assistant asks clarifying questions to resolve user intent before answering. Three cooperating agents identify candidate interpretations, request clarification, and generate answers grounded in the resolved intent and visual evidence. A bounded, cost-aware policy balances expected information gain against user effort while limiting clarification turns and question complexity. Referclaw learns from interaction through intent-specific skill updates and process-reward-guided policy optimization with agent-level credit assignment. Evaluations on augmented egocentric QA benchmarks demonstrate improved answer accuracy and joint answer-and-grounding performance over existing baselines, alongside zero-shot transfer across datasets. These findings support interactive clarification as a practical approach to resolving online user intent in egocentric streaming assistance.

</details>


### [80] [Hierarchical Incremental Beta Splatting for Online Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08288)
*Linus Franke*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HIBS 将 Beta 核引入在线增量场景重建，以置信度感知初始化、误差驱动重定位和层次化细节表示兼顾重建质量与速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线 3D Gaussian Splatting 每个关键帧的优化迭代有限，初始化不佳和细节缺失难以修正；高斯核较软，构建实体表面需要大量重叠图元。

Method: 利用几何可观测性与多视角深度一致性初始化 Beta 核的不透明度和形状；根据光度误差将失效图元重定位到误差热点；利用有界支撑进行精确图块剔除，并通过闭式保形合并构建 LOD 层次。

Result: 在 Mip-NeRF 360 的即时重建协议下，PSNR 最高提升 0.8 dB，优化时间最高减少 40%。

Conclusion: Beta 核的可调形状与置信度感知增量重建相结合，可改善在线重建的质量和效率，并支持实时渲染。

Abstract: Online 3D Gaussian Splatting reconstructs a scene during capture: each keyframe spawns primitives once and refines them for only a few dozen iterations, leaving little chance to fix poor initialization or missing detail. Gaussians are also inherently soft, needing many overlaps for solid surfaces. We present Hierarchical Incremental Beta Splatting (HIBS), which brings beta kernels to incremental reconstruction. Their shape parameter interpolates between a soft, Gaussian-like profile and a flat-topped, near-opaque surfel, acting as a confidence dial for geometry. Our confidence-aware spawning uses geometric observability and multi-view depth agreement to initialize both opacity and shape, producing solid primitives where geometry is verifiable and soft, correctable primitives where it is not. As online densification, error-driven relocation recycles stale primitives into photometric-error hotspots. Bounded beta kernel support enables exact tile culling and faster rasterization, and a closed-form, shape-preserving merging formulation provides a level-of-detail (LOD) hierarchy for fast optimization and real-time rendering. On Mip-NeRF 360 in the on-the-fly protocol, HIBS improves PSNR by up to 0.8 dB while reducing optimization time by up to 40%. Project page: https://linus-franke.com/hibs/

</details>


### [81] [InfMasking: A Unified Variational Framework for Synergistic Multimodal Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07702)
*Liangjian Wen,Qun Dai,Jiang Duan,Yong Dai,Jianzhuang Liu,Dongkai Wang,Jun Wang,Fuyuan Cao,Zenglin Xu,Zhao Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InfMasking通过无限随机掩码和一致性学习，促进多模态模型利用跨模态协同信息，并在所评测基准上提升预测性能及交互相关指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有融合模块可能依赖优势模态或模态间共享信息，忽略只有联合观察多个模态才能获得的协同信息。

Method: 融合前随机掩码各模态特征，使不同部分观测下的融合表示与参考目标保持一致：无监督训练采用未掩码融合视图，有监督训练采用任务标签的类别代理。针对无限掩码上的期望，将掩码融合表示建模为高斯分布，并优化闭式变分界；在该模型下，其总体矩间隙由掩码诱导的协方差控制。

Result: 在所评测基准上，预测性能提高；学习到的模态特定表示中，任务标签协同信息的平均PID估计更高，训练后预测器的InterSHAP交互归因也更高。摘要未提供具体数值。

Conclusion: 随机掩码下的一致性约束可减少对优势模态或共享信息的依赖，促进学习部分观测下仍有意义的跨模态交互；具体适用范围和增益大小需结合全文及实验验证。

Abstract: Multimodal models are expected to exploit synergistic information, that is, information available only when multiple modalities are observed together. In practice, however, a fusion module can fit the training objective by relying on a dominant modality or on information that modalities share, and the synergy between modalities is often left unused. We propose InfMasking, a unified multimodal learning framework based on Infinite Masking. Before fusion, InfMasking randomly masks the features of each modality; each sampled mask produces a different partial combination of cross-modal evidence and a fused representation of the corresponding modality interactions. All masked fused representations are required to remain consistent with a reference target: the unmasked fused view when training is unsupervised, and the class proxy of the task label when labels are available. Maintaining this consistency across diverse partial combinations discourages reliance on a dominant modality or on shared information alone, and drives the fusion module to learn cross-modal interaction patterns that remain meaningful under partial observations. Since the objective is an expectation over infinitely many masks, we model the masked fused representations as Gaussian and optimize closed-form variational bounds, whose population-moment gaps are controlled by the masking-induced covariance under this model. On the evaluated benchmarks, InfMasking improves predictive performance and yields higher mean PID estimates of task-label synergy in learned modality-specific representations, alongside higher InterSHAP interaction attribution in the trained predictors. Code is available at https://github.com/brightest66/InfMasking.

</details>


### [82] [ManimCode: Learning Visual Self-Correction for Educational Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08294)
*Zhengyang Liang,Lizi Liao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 ManimData 和 ManimCode，利用渲染反馈与修订轨迹训练单个 4B 模型，提升教育动画代码的生成、审查与修复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成功渲染不代表动画不存在视觉缺陷、解释缺失或内容错误；现有迭代框架常依赖大型专有模型，而现有数据集缺少质量验证及反馈修订轨迹。

Method: 通过检索 API 文档并迭代生成、执行、审查和修订代码，构建包含 24,034 条经渲染验证和质量筛选记录的 ManimData；对轨迹进行角色条件监督微调，训练 4B ManimCode 执行编写、审查和修订；再利用自生成状态、渲染结果和无参考质量反馈，通过强化学习训练修订角色。

Result: 在 ManimData-test 和 ManimBench 上，渲染成功率分别由未训练 4B 模型的 52.6% 和 82.0% 提升至 96.6% 和 97.0%；视频质量分数分别由 2.48 和 3.00 升至 2.94 和 3.44。接入两个长视频系统后，完成数分别由 19/117 和 53/239 升至 107/117 和 239/239。

Conclusion: 反馈轨迹监督训练结合强化学习，能够增强小模型修正动画程序和支持长视频生成的能力；摘要未说明质量评分细则或完整消融结果。

Abstract: Manim supports precise, programmatic video generation, but a successfully rendered educational animation can still have visual defects, missing explanations, or incorrect content. Rendering does not directly identify the code changes needed to correct these visual failures, so feedback-driven revision is needed. Existing agentic frameworks use iterative rendering and revision but typically rely on large models, often proprietary. Existing Manim datasets primarily contain final programs and often lack quality verification and the trajectories of feedback and revisions needed to learn this refinement process. We introduce ManimData and ManimCode, a data and training framework for feedback-driven educational animation generation. We construct ManimData through a render-guided pipeline that iteratively generates, executes, reviews, and revises Manim programs with retrieved API documentation. Its 24,034 render-verified, quality-filtered records preserve intermediate programs, execution errors, visual critiques, and corrective revisions. We use role-conditioned supervised fine-tuning on these trajectories to train ManimCode, a single 4B model, to write, review, and revise animation programs. To improve correction of errors that differ from those in the collected trajectories, we then train the Reviser with reinforcement learning on self-generated states, using rendering outcomes and reference-free quality feedback. ManimCode achieves 96.6% and 97.0% render success on ManimData-test and ManimBench, compared with 52.6% and 82.0% for the untrained 4B model under the same inference setup, while also improving video quality scores from 2.48 to 2.94 and from 3.00 to 3.44. Integrating ManimCode into two long-form educational video generation systems increases completed videos from 19/117 to 107/117 and from 53/239 to 239/239, alongside improvements in educational effectiveness and visual quality.

</details>


### [83] [InstantHand: Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian Splatting for Animatable Hand Avatar Reconstruction from a Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08330)
*Chanho Kim,Muhammad Salman Ali,Kyeonghwan Gwak,Muhammad Shaheryar,Incheol Park,Jeongwan On,Seungryul Baek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InstantHand 从单张图像快速重建可动画的三维高斯手部数字化身，无需测试时优化，并兼顾轮廓与外观保真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手部数字化身重建依赖多视角采集或逐主体优化，扩展性有限；图像平均损失对透明度泄漏监督不足，且颜色回归可能偏离输入图像的真实外观。

Method: 利用现成的 MANO、相机和掩码估计，通过前馈网络构建三维高斯手部化身；采用软 Alpha 边界带正则化强化轮廓监督，并依据各高斯的渲染贡献进行可靠性门控外观迁移，对未观测区域使用以全局源身份为条件的学习先验补全。

Result: 化身构建耗时 2.84 秒，渲染速度为 161 FPS；在 InterHand2.6M 上达到逐主体优化基线的表现，在 HanCo 零样本评估中保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 该方法在无需测试时优化的情况下实现快速、可动画的单图手部重建，并通过针对性的轮廓和外观处理改善重建质量；摘要未提供具体精度指标或消融结果。

Abstract: Hand avatar reconstruction typically relies on multi-view capture or per-subject optimization, limiting its scalability. We introduce InstantHand, a feed-forward framework that constructs an animatable 3D Gaussian hand avatar from a single image using off-the-shelf MANO, camera, and mask estimates, without test-time optimization. Image-averaged losses provide insufficient supervision for alpha leakage, which thickens thin finger contours. To address this issue, we propose a Soft Alpha Band regularizer that concentrates supervision on silhouette boundaries. Furthermore, colors regressed from image features can drift toward a learned prior even in observed regions. Our Reliability-gated Appearance Transfer preserves observed appearance by transferring source colors according to each Gaussian's rendering contribution, while completing unobserved regions using the learned prior conditioned on global source identity. InstantHand constructs an avatar in 2.84 seconds and renders at 161 FPS, matching per-subject-optimized baselines on InterHand2.6M and remaining competitive in zero-shot evaluation on HanCo.

</details>


### [84] [4D Point Splatting for mmWave Radar Micro-Doppler Differentiable Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08342)
*Adnan Armouti,Guanyue Qian,Rajalakshmi Nandakumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出物理驱动的可微毫米波雷达渲染器4D Point Splatting，通过拟合真实动态场景生成雷达原始复数回波，显著缩小仿真到真实数据的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫米波雷达的人体感知任务依赖真实训练数据，但数据规模远落后于视觉和语言领域；现有仿真、生成和静态场景逆渲染方法难以拟合真实运动人体捕获数据。

Method: 利用同步相机初始化房间和人体，将场景表示为具有材质、法线和速度的有向点，并投射至距离与多普勒单元，生成原始复数回波；通过可微优化拟合真实采集序列，再使用常规信号处理生成下游模型所需表示。

Result: 在HuPR和RT-Pose分别拟合235和100条序列；距离-方位图中位相关系数分别为0.940和0.830，距离-多普勒为0.829和0.695，微多普勒为0.988和0.966。RT-Pose上，合成数据训练的姿态模型在真实数据上达到21.9厘米MPJPE，真实数据训练为16.7厘米，RF-Genesis基线为127.6厘米。单张H100拟合每条序列分别需5.7和12.9分钟。

Conclusion: 4DPS实现了高保真、可扩展的动态毫米波逆渲染，有望支持人体感知任务的合成数据生成和场景编辑增强；其真实任务性能仍与真实数据训练存在差距。

Abstract: Millimeter-wave radar resolves the micrometer displacements of a moving body as micro-Doppler, enabling gait, gesture, activity, pose and vital-sign sensing. Each task relies on a downstream model trained on real data, yet radar datasets lag orders of magnitude behind vision and language. Brute-force collection will not close that gap, and existing synthesis methods (forward-only simulators, learned generators and static-scene inverse renderers) cannot fit a real capture of a moving body, leaving a sim2real gap. We present 4D Point Splatting (4DPS), a physics-based differentiable renderer that fits an explicit moving scene to a real capture. Using the synchronized camera to initialize the room and body, we represent the scene as oriented points carrying material, normal and velocity, and splat each point into range and Doppler bins. Our output is the raw complex return a real radar records, so we apply the same signal processing on the rendered data to obtain any representation for training any SOTA downstream model. We fit 235 sequences on HuPR and 100 on RT-Pose, two datasets with different radars. Our reconstructions set the state of the art, with median correlations of 0.940 and 0.830 on range-azimuth, 0.829 and 0.695 on range-Doppler, and 0.988 and 0.966 on micro-Doppler. Our renders also set the state of the art in downstream sim2real gap. On RT-Pose, a pose model trained on them reaches 21.9 cm MPJPE on real data, against 16.7 cm for real training and 127.6 cm for the RF-Genesis baseline, where both the rendered- and real-trained models learn nearly the same features. Our custom CUDA kernels fit a sequence in 5.7 minutes on HuPR and 12.9 on RT-Pose on one H100, making dataset-scale inverse rendering of mmWave radar possible for the first time. This work can enable synthetic data generation for any human-centered downstream task and model, and scene editing for data augmentation.

</details>


### [85] [USB Gym: A Gymnasium Testbed for Closed-Loop Ultrasound View Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08352)
*Thanapong Intharah,Zhichao Gao,Yiliang Liu,Hao Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 作者提出超声探头导航测试平台 USB Gym；组合现有方法后，探头平均距目标仍有 13.7 mm，显示该任务尚未得到充分解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超声导航组件通常在不同模拟器中分别评估，缺乏对组合导航系统能否有效到达目标视图的统一检验。

Method: 基于公开患者 CT 构建超声模拟测试平台，并利用网络从 CT 标注生成器官纹理。导航系统包含可替换的器官分割器、控制器和奖励函数；在六名留出患者的相同 60 个回合中评估 20 种已发表方法，仅以表现可检测地更好的方法替换原组件。随后在 20 例新 CT 上比较组合与原系统，并以仅在模拟中可获得精确探头位姿的预言机作为参考。

Result: 新 CT 上初始平均距离为 16.0 mm，位姿预言机降至 1.5 mm。组合确定时，仅结构相似性（SSIM）奖励略有且可检测的改进；采用该奖励的组合成为基准基线，最终平均距离为 13.7 mm，约实现预言机距离改善的 16%。

Conclusion: 未经针对性调优的已发表方法与作者方法组合后，仍无法将探头引导至目标视图附近。结果为探索性发现，平台支持接入新方法，且尚未进行硬件测试。

Abstract: Background and Objective: Our main goal is ultrasound guidance: moving a probe until its image matches a target view. Published methods for each part of a guide are evaluated in separate simulators. We asked whether these methods, combined into one guide, are good enough.
  Methods: We built USB Gym, a testbed whose standard engine simulates ultrasound from public patient CT, with a network drawing organ textures from CT labels. Its guide has three replaceable parts (slots), an organ segmenter, a controller and a reward, which received 20 published methods, each run on the same 60 episodes of six held-out patients. In each slot, a published method replaced ours only if detectably better on these patients. The better of this combination and our original guide on 20 new CTs is the benchmark baseline. A pose oracle, reading the exact probe pose available only in simulation, set the reference. The results below are exploratory.
  Results: On these CTs, the probe started on average 16.0 mm from the target view; the pose oracle brought it to 1.5 mm. Of the results known when the combination was fixed, only an image-similarity reward (SSIM) was slightly but detectably better, so the combination was our guide with that reward. This combination was the benchmark baseline, ending at 13.7 mm (16% of the oracle's progress).
  Conclusions: Combining untuned published methods with ours did not guide the probe near the target view; the task remains under-explored. The testbed accepts new methods; no guide was tested on hardware.

</details>


### [86] [OraRefer: Segmentation-Guided Multi-Photograph Fusion for Oral Cancer Screening with Guaranteed Patient-Level Sensitivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08360)
*Rupam Mukherjee,Mehakdeep Singh,Shirin Dasgupta,Subhamoy Mandal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OraRefer融合患者多张口腔照片及风险因素进行筛查，并通过有限样本校准设定灵敏度目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型主要分类单张照片，但实际筛查需针对患者综合多张照片作出转诊决策，并控制疾病漏检比例。

Method: 采用分割引导的基础模型，结合预测病灶判别每张照片；融合患者全部照片及风险因素；利用有限样本校准转诊阈值。模型自动定位口腔，推理无需人工标注。

Result: 在714名患者的3000张照片上，患者级融合AUROC为0.919，优于基于注意力的多实例学习。灵敏度目标为90%时，检出95%的口腔潜在恶性病变或癌症患者，特异度为0.610；61名癌症患者中漏检3人。

Conclusion: 患者级多图像融合有助于口腔筛查；无需重新训练即可使用本地校准样本调整转诊阈值。摘要未提供保证的置信水平或外部验证结果。

Abstract: Oral cancer and the oral potentially malignant disorders (OPMD) that can precede it are visible on the oral mucosa, which makes smartphone photographs a promising tool for screening. Deep learning models for this task classify individual photographs. However, screening refers patients, not photographs: a patient is usually photographed several times, and the referral decision must miss no more than a set share of patients with disease. We propose OraRefer, a patient-level screening model that reads each photograph with a segmentation-guided foundation model that conditions its class decision on the predicted lesion, combines all photographs of a patient with risk factors, and refers through a threshold with a finite-sample sensitivity guarantee. The model localizes the mouth itself, so inference needs no annotation. On 3000 photographs of 714 patients, patient-level fusion reaches an AUROC of 0.919 and outperforms attention-based multiple-instance learning. At a 90% sensitivity target, OraRefer detects 95% of patients with OPMD or cancer and misses 3 of 61 patients with cancer, at a specificity of 0.610. Because the threshold is calibrated without retraining, each clinic can set its own guaranteed operating point on a local calibration sample.

</details>


### [87] [Adaptive Low-Light Enhancement for Road Anomaly Detection: A Multi-Architecture Benchmark and Edge Deployment Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08393)
*Obed Honour Eje,Oluwatosin Kolade,Glory Bagai,Taliha Abiodun Folorunso*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出基于帧亮度的轻量级自适应路由，在低照度道路异常检测中按需选择增强策略，以减轻统一增强带来的性能损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶道路异常检测需应对黑暗、降雨和哈马丹沙尘霾；统一使用低照度增强可能在黄昏光照下损害检测性能。

Method: 基准评测YOLOv8n、YOLOv12n、RT-DETR和Faster R-CNN在具有物理依据的光照与天气退化条件下的表现；依据每帧Rec. 601亮度，在不增强、中等强度SCI增强和去噪加高强度SCI增强之间路由，无需训练，并在NVIDIA Jetson Nano上验证完整流程。

Result: 统一应用SCI在黄昏级光照下使mAP@50下降16.1%至30.7%；自适应路由挽回了大部分黄昏场景下的性能损失，同时保留低照度增强收益。摘要未提供路由方案的具体提升数值或硬件性能指标。

Conclusion: 按帧亮度选择增强策略比统一增强更有利于兼顾黄昏和低照度场景；完整方案已在边缘硬件上验证，但摘要不足以判断实时性或其他详细部署性能。

Abstract: Road anomaly detection systems on autonomous vehicles must function reliably under degraded visual conditions including darkness, rain, and Harmattan dust haze. This paper benchmarks four object detection architectures, YOLOv8n, YOLOv12n, RT-DETR, and Faster R-CNN, under physically grounded lighting and weather degradation, and shows that uniform application of the SCI low-light enhancement network is actively harmful at dusk-level lighting (-16.1% to -30.7% mAP@50). We propose a lightweight, training-free luminance-adaptive router that selects between no enhancement, moderate SCI, and denoising plus strong SCI based on per-frame Rec. 601 luminance, recovering most of the dusk penalty while preserving low-light gains, and we validate the full pipeline on NVIDIA Jetson Nano edge hardware.

</details>


### [88] [SM4RT: Cascaded Feed-forward Model for 4D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08436)
*Pang-Chi Lo,Jhao-Syun Lai,Tsung-Wei Ke*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SM4RT通过级联三维几何重建与运动估计，从RGB视频重建动态场景，并利用弱监督缓解运动标签稀疏问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈方法通常将几何重建和运动估计分开处理；单一坐标系下的运动预测还需同时考虑相机运动，容易产生歧义，而真实场景运动标签稀疏。

Method: 采用多任务级联前馈架构，先重建三维几何，再以预测结构辅助运动估计；在各像素对应的相机坐标系中表示其三维运动，并利用另一时间步的标注点位置，对无标签点运动施加弱监督约束。

Result: 在Kubric、Waymo和Point Odyssey三个动态场景数据集上，运动估计在多数指标优于已有基线；在五个动态场景数据集上的几何重建表现与最先进方法具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 级联结合几何重建与运动估计可避免两项任务间的性能权衡，并通过改进几何推理提升运动估计；摘要未提供具体数值或消融结果。

Abstract: We introduce SM4RT, a multi-task cascade feed-forward model for dynamic scene reconstruction from RGB videos. While existing feed-forward approaches typically address geometry reconstruction and motion estimation separately--and often predict motions in a single coordinate frame that must account for camera movement--SM4RT solves these tasks sequentially by first reconstructing 3D geometry and then bootstrapping motion estimation from the predicted structure. By expressing each pixel's 3D motion in its corresponding camera frame, SM4RT reduces ambiguity arising from camera motion. To address the sparsity of real-world motion labels, SM4RT further incorporates a weakly supervised loss that regularizes unlabeled point motions using labeled point positions at the other time step. We evaluate motion estimation on three dynamic-scene datasets--Kubric, Waymo, and Point Odyssey--and SM4RT achieves the best performance on most metrics compared with prior baselines. We additionally benchmark geometry reconstruction on five dynamic scene datasets and show that SM4RT achieves competitive performance with state-of-the-art methods. Overall, our unified framework demonstrates that combining geometry reconstruction and motion estimation in a cascade not only avoids performance trade-offs but also enhances motion estimation through improved geometric reasoning.

</details>


### [89] [Edge-Assisted Crowdsensing for Explainable Cyclist Safety Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08441)
*Hongsen Cao,Chia-Yen Chiang,Mona Jaber,Meihui Wang,Yasmin Fathy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出边缘辅助众包系统，结合视觉语言模型和可解释提升机，连续预测骑行路线的感知安全评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统事故统计和多模型检测流程难以扩展到路线级连续安全监测；自动驾驶车辆也增加了混合交通环境的复杂性。

Method: 车把摄像头采集街景图像；视觉语言模型通过单次提示提取结构化交通属性；可解释提升机融合这些属性、OpenStreetMap道路基础设施及骑行运动学数据，预测众包的1—9分安全评分。

Result: 在伦敦两条路线的1,362张标注图像上，五折交叉验证得到72.2%的二分类准确率和1.011的平均绝对误差，与黑箱集成模型的准确率差距不到1个百分点。特征归因显示，物理隔离车道为+0.97分、标线车道为+0.70分、施工现场为-0.65分。标注者一致性alpha为0.4695。最快自托管视觉语言模型每帧耗时2.49秒，CPU评分耗时0.4毫秒；重新编码后，以20秒间隔服务1,000名骑行者需54 Mbit/s，准确率降低1.4个百分点。

Conclusion: 该系统展示了可解释、可扩展的骑行感知安全监测潜力，可为基础设施评估提供量化依据；但结果基于有限路线和中等标注一致性，特征归因不应直接视为因果效应。

Abstract: Cyclist fatality rates remain high despite policy support for active travel, and autonomous vehicles add complexity to mixed traffic. Neither crash statistics nor multi-model detection pipelines scale to continuous monitoring of perceived safety along a route. We present an edge-assisted crowdsensing system: handlebar cameras supply street-level frames, a vision-language model (VLM) turns each frame into structured traffic attributes through a single prompt, and an explainable boosting machine (EBM) combines them with OpenStreetMap road infrastructure and on-bike kinematics to predict a crowdsourced 1-9 safety score. Extraction belongs at the network edge: the fastest self-hosted model needs 2.49 s per frame and scoring takes 0.4 ms on a CPU. On 1,362 annotated frames from two London routes, the EBM regressor reaches 72.2 percent binary accuracy and a mean absolute error of 1.011 under five-fold cross-validation. It is within one accuracy point of black-box ensembles. Its per-feature shape functions give exact attribution: physical lane separation is worth +0.97 rating points, a painted lane +0.70 and a construction site -0.65. Planners can therefore price an infrastructure change in rating points, and the results enter cost-benefit analysis directly. We also report inter-annotator agreement (alpha = 0.4695), an attribute audit, and a sensitivity analysis linking attribute error to accuracy. With re-encoding, a 20 s sampling interval serves 1,000 riders at 54 Mbit/s at a cost of 1.4 accuracy points, making continuous monitoring practical.

</details>


### [90] [EgoGeneralist: Unifying Egocentric Action-Video Tasks through Video-Native Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08477)
*Yanlin Jin,Bowen Yang,Cheng Zhang,Yifan Yang,Zhe Li,Jianfei Cai,Kai Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EgoGeneralist 将预训练视频生成模型适配为统一框架，联合处理第一人称视频与度量尺度三维人体动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频模型具有较强的视觉先验，但缺乏对度量尺度三维人体动作的原生表示，难以统一动作控制、姿态估计与联合生成。

Method: 通过角色自适应动作渲染器，将度量运动转换为适合视频模型处理的表示，使其既可作为空间控制信号，也可作为可恢复的生成目标；利用跨流动作—视频耦合和轻量化适配，建立运动与视觉动态之间的对应关系，同时保留预训练视觉先验。

Result: 该框架覆盖五项动作—视频任务；仅使用受控实验室录制数据进行适配后，仍能泛化到多样的未见场景和交互，并实现有效的动作控制与视觉质量。摘要未提供具体定量结果。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，预训练视频模型的视觉知识可与度量尺度三维人体运动相结合，支持跨任务的动作—视频建模与场景泛化。

Abstract: Modern video models possess strong visual priors, yet do not natively represent human action in metric 3D space. We present EgoGeneralist, which adapts pretrained video generators to unify action-conditioned egocentric video generation, pose estimation, and joint action-video generation. Our role-adaptive action renderer provides video-native representations for multidirectional action-video learning, making metric motion usable as both a spatial control signal and a recoverable generation target. Cross-stream action-video coupling establishes close correspondence between metric motion and visual dynamics through lightweight adaptation while preserving the pretrained visual prior. Together, these designs establish a generalist framework spanning five action-video tasks. Despite adaptation only on controlled laboratory recordings, EgoGeneralist generalizes to diverse unseen scenes and interactions with effective action control and visual quality, bridging pretrained visual knowledge and metric 3D human motion.

</details>


### [91] [Where and Why is Grounded Reasoning Useful?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08485)
*David Yan,Le Xue,Forrest Huang,Qiuyi Zhang,Dustin Tran,Andrew M. Dai,Yinfei Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 具身定位推理主要有助于计数及难以用语言描述的视觉引用；作者提出 BrickVision 以评估后者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对推理过程中输出视觉位置坐标的实际收益及其局限缺乏系统分析。

Method: 提出思维链分析方法，衡量模型在推理轨迹中对所输出定位信息的使用程度；对定位推理训练方案进行消融，并使用合成任务和多种视觉基准评估。

Result: 定位推理改善了计数，但在广泛的空间推理和感知基准上，相比无坐标定位的基线没有显著收益；合成任务表明，其在需要难以用语言描述的视觉引用时最有用。

Conclusion: 许多现有视觉推理基准仅靠可用文本描述的引用即可解决，可能无法充分评估定位推理；作者因此提出需要难以描述的视觉引用才能解决的 BrickVision 基准。

Abstract: Grounded reasoning, where a model outputs text coordinates of relevant locations in chain-of-thought reasoning, is a common strategy for solving complex visual reasoning problems. However, the benefits and limitations of grounded reasoning are understudied. We study where and why grounded reasoning is most useful. To do so, we introduce a chain-of-thought analysis method to measure how much a model uses its outputted grounding in its thinking trace and perform a detailed ablation study of a grounded reasoning training recipe. We find that grounded reasoning improves counting but shows no significant gain over baselines without coordinate grounding across a wide range of spatial reasoning and perception benchmarks. Using several synthetic tasks, we show that grounded reasoning is most useful when a task requires undescribable references, which are visual concepts that are difficult to describe in language. However, we observe that many existing visual reasoning benchmarks are solvable with only describable text references. To close this gap, we introduce BrickVision, a new benchmark that requires undescribable visual references to solve.

</details>


### [92] [InnerLogic-Surg: An Open Surgical Foundation Model via Continual Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07883)
*Kun Yuan,Hao Ding,Yiqing Shen,Gasper Podobnik,Benjamin Killeen,Tito Porras,Axel Krieger,Mathias Unberath*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InnerLogic-Surg 通过大规模手术图像持续预训练构建开放式视觉编码器，在20项手术任务的线性探测评测中均取得最佳表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手术图像分析依赖自然图像预训练编码器，或基于较小数据集从头训练的专用模型；需要可泛化、更强的手术领域视觉骨干。

Method: 基于 Transformer 图像编码器和 DINOv3 预训练阶段，使用来自11,965段手术视频的数百万张图像进行持续预训练；引入可学习的特征上采样模块，以较低 token 成本支持高分辨率密集预测。模型规模为2100万至3亿参数。

Result: 在涵盖工作流程、器械与解剖结构识别、分割、检测和单目深度估计的基准中，相同架构条件下，最大模型在全部20项手术任务上线性探测表现最佳。

Conclusion: 手术领域持续预训练可学习跨术式、解剖区域和任务类型迁移的表征，为术式专用表征学习提供有潜力且可扩展的替代方案；摘要未提供具体性能数值。

Abstract: Vision foundation models are the backbone of modern surgical image analysis, yet most surgical systems still rely on encoders pretrained on natural images or on specialist models trained from scratch on comparatively small datasets. Pretraining encoders on large amounts of surgery-specific data has the potential to generally enhance the performance of surgical image analysis methods simply by providing stronger backbones. Here we present InnerLogic-Surg, an open family of surgical vision encoders (21M--300M parameters) obtained by continually pretraining a transformer-based image encoder on millions of surgical images curated from 11,965 internet-sourced surgical videos spanning ~2,900 hours and more than fifteen procedure types. Our training recipe inherits DINOv3's pretraining stage to preserve representation ability after surgical adaptation, and additionally applies a learned feature-upsampling module for high-resolution dense prediction at low token cost. Across a benchmark covering workflow, instrument and anatomy recognition, segmentation, detection, and monocular depth, under the same architecture, the largest InnerLogic-Surg model achieves the best linear-probe performance in all 20 surgical tasks. We demonstrate that surgical continual pretraining yields transferable representations that generalize across procedures, anatomical regions, and task types, showing our surgical image encoders as a promising and scalable alternative to the procedure-specific representation learning approaches that have traditionally dominated surgical computer vision.

</details>


### [93] [Do You Miss the Nights We Spent Crafting Figures? Rethinking Teaser and Framework Figures in the Age of Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08486)
*Xiaoqi Zhao,Youwei Pang,Kailai Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究分析371张科研动机图和框架图：生成式AI辅助修改提高了解释清晰度，但略微降低审美一致性，科研图示仍需作者主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨生成式AI介入科研图示设计后，如何影响图示的解释清晰度与有目的的视觉表达。

Method: 回顾200篇论文中的371张图，包括跨四个时期的160篇配额主样本和40篇目的性风格对照样本；由单一多模态评判模型从六个维度对每张图重复评分三次，并对全部图进行受源图约束的修改与保真检查。

Result: 原图评分未随时期呈现一致下降趋势；371张修改图中369张通过记录的保真检查。五分制下，修改后平均清晰度提高0.428分，审美一致性下降0.111分；主样本中动机图的六维平均得分增益大于框架图。

Conclusion: AI可辅助探索和改善科研图示，但增加说明并不能自动解决文字层级和视觉简洁性问题；作者应负责解释目的、构图及科学关系。结果受单一模型评判和样本选择限制。

Abstract: Scientific figures give research ideas a visual form through choices of abstraction, composition, and emphasis. Generative AI offers new ways to realize these choices, raising questions about explanatory clarity and purposeful visual expression. We examine these questions through a visual retrospective of 371 motivation and framework figures from 200 papers. The corpus comprises a quota-based main sample of 160 papers spanning four temporal periods and a separate purposive style-contrast sample of 40 papers. A single multimodal judge evaluates six dimensions of communication and design, with three repeated judgments per figure. We attempt source-constrained revisions of all 371 figures, of which 369 pass the recorded fidelity checks. Original-figure ratings show no uniform decline across periods. Revisions increase mean clarity ratings by 0.428 points and decrease aesthetic coherence ratings by 0.111 points on a five-point scale. Gains in the six-dimension average are larger for main-sample motivation figures than for frameworks. Case analyses show that added explanation can clarify comparisons while leaving typographic hierarchy and visual economy unresolved. These model-based findings support researcher-led design, with AI assisting exploration and authors retaining responsibility for explanatory purpose, composition, and scientific relationships. The accompanying atlas and evaluation records are available at https://ntu-ai4x.github.io/Rethinking_Figures/index_en.html.

</details>


### [94] [Structure from Collision via Hypothesis-Space Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07900)
*Takuhiro Kaneko*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于显式结构假设的碰撞重建方法，从多视角碰撞形变中推断物体内部空腔，并改善未来运动预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内部空腔在静态表面不可直接观测；传统密度表示中，表面匹配梯度难以定位空腔，空洞内及附近的密度梯度也可能微弱或缺乏信息。

Method: 将问题转化为假设空间推理：先利用仅前向的物质点法（MPM）仿真，对编码结构先验的显式空腔假设进行无梯度全局优化；再将选定假设表示为拉格朗日粒子系统，通过可微MPM进行梯度局部优化。

Result: 在SfC基准上，相比基于密度的SfC基线，该方法改善了内部结构重建和未来预测；拓扑压力测试与鲁棒性分析考察了适用范围和局限。摘要未给出具体数值。

Conclusion: 显式结构假设结合全局搜索与可微局部细化，能够缓解隐式密度优化中空腔定位困难的问题；其效果和适用范围仍受结构假设及测试条件约束。

Abstract: Knowledge of the hidden interior structure of an object, including the presence and location of cavities, is essential for predictive simulation, robotic manipulation, and nondestructive inspection. However, such cavities leave no direct signature on the static visible surface, making collision-induced deformation one of the few cues used to infer their presence. Structure from Collision (SfC) addresses this problem by recovering the hidden interior structure of an object from multiview observations of a collision event. SfC is challenging for two reasons: hidden cavities are only indirectly observable through surface motion, and voids correspond to the absence of material, making direct optimization using standard density representations difficult. The density-based SfC baseline inherits both difficulties. Surface-matching gradients do not directly indicate the cavity location, while density gradients can be weak or uninformative inside or near void regions. Therefore, we reformulate SfC as hypothesis-space reasoning, representing hidden cavities as explicit structural hypotheses whose variables encode available structural assumptions rather than relying on implicit density updates. A global stage performs gradient-free optimization over this hypothesis space using forward-only material point method (MPM) simulation. A local stage then refines the selected hypothesis as a Lagrangian particle system through gradient-based optimization using differentiable MPM simulation. On the SfC benchmark, our method improves hidden-structure reconstruction and future prediction over the density-based SfC baseline. Interior-topology stress tests and robustness analyses characterize its scope and limitations. The project page is available at https://www.kecl.ntt.co.jp/people/kaneko.takuhiro/projects/sfc-hypothesis/.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [95] [Interactive Backdoor Attacks in LLM-Based Proactive Dialogue Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06969)
*Zhiyang Xia,Yuming Shang,Xi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出通过污染微调对话植入交互式后门，使模型在触发后跨多轮引导对话，且常规效用大体保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型后门研究主要关注触发后生成单次指定回复，缺少对目标导向、多轮交互操纵的研究。

Method: 利用投毒微调对话植入触发条件下的引导策略偏差，分别设计直接攻击、沿预定轨迹推进的受约束渐进攻击，以及适应不同用户续接的无约束渐进攻击。

Result: 在心理咨询场景下，基于两个模型和两个对话数据集的实验表明，三种攻击在各自设定中均有效，同时大体保留正常效用；所测数据过滤器保留了几乎全部投毒对话，三种后续防御基线仍存在较高残余攻击成功率。

Conclusion: 多轮主动对话系统面临超越单次回复操纵的后门风险，所测试防御措施不足以充分消除此风险；摘要未提供具体量化结果。

Abstract: LLM-based proactive dialogue systems provide goal-directed support through sustained interaction. Fine-tuning their underlying models on externally supplied dialogue data introduces backdoor risks. Existing LLM backdoor studies primarily focus on eliciting an attacker-specified response upon trigger activation. We present an interactive backdoor attack framework that uses training-data poisoning to steer post-trigger, multi-turn interactions toward an attacker-specified objective. Poisoned fine-tuning dialogues implant a trigger-conditioned guidance-policy bias that ordinary user utterances can activate, without deployment-time prompt modification or further attacker intervention. Specifically, we instantiate three complementary attacks under progressively expanded post-trigger interaction settings: (1) Direct Attack, which targets an immediate response deviation upon trigger activation, retaining the conventional trigger--response setting; (2) Constrained Progressive Attack, which progressively advances the attack objective along a predefined multi-turn trajectory; and (3) Unconstrained Progressive Attack, which extends target-directed steering to open-ended interactions with varied user continuations. Using psychological counseling as a representative setting, experiments on two LLM backbones and two dialogue datasets show that all three attacks effectively achieve their objectives under their respective interaction settings while largely preserving normal utility. Separate defense evaluations show that the tested training-data filters retain nearly all poisoned dialogues, while three post-admission baselines leave substantial residual attack-specific success.

</details>


### [96] [On the Opportunities and Challenges of Prefill-to-Autoregressive Cross-Model Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06976)
*Zhenyi Shen,Junru Lu,Wei Liu,Jiangnan Ye,Jiazheng Li,Di Yin,Xing Sun,Tameem Adel,Yali Du,Lin Gui,Yulan He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: P​​Latour 实现跨模型潜在通信，并提出 PACT-Check 区分真正的发送方信息贡献与桥接层自身的适配收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型与小模型协作有望兼顾能力和推理成本，但现有系统缺乏独立评估工具；任务性能提升本身不能证明接收模型利用了发送方信息。

Method: 构建开源 PACT 框架 PLatour，通过轻量级、零初始化的线性桥连接冻结模型，直接使用任务奖励训练桥接层，并支持混合注意力架构；通过对照实验和 PACT-Check 量化通信贡献。

Result: 在生成任务上显著优于开源基线；使用小得多的发送模型，在共同数学和编程基准上超过 Mostik 报告的结果。但部分任务的收益可由无信息发送信号、自通信或仅训练接收模型复现。

Conclusion: 可训练桥接层可能充当参数高效适配机制，而非利用有用的发送方信息；评估 PACT 系统时应通过受控实验验证通信的实际贡献。

Abstract: Prefill-to-Autoregressive Cross-model Transfer (PACT), a form of latent communication between language models, combines a larger model's prompt representations with a smaller model's efficient generation, offering a promising way to balance capability and inference cost. However, existing systems provide limited support for independently evaluating large-small model collaboration, and improved task performance alone does not establish whether the receiver benefits from the sender's information. We introduce PLatour, an open-source PACT framework that connects frozen models through lightweight, zero-initialized linear bridges trained directly with task rewards and supports hybrid-attention architectures. PLatour significantly outperforms open-source baselines on generation tasks and surpasses Mostik's reported results on shared mathematics and coding benchmarks using a substantially smaller sender. However, controlled experiments show that the gains on some tasks can be reproduced by uninformative sender signals, self-communication, or receiver-only training. We find that trainable bridges can act as parameter-efficient adaptation mechanisms without exploiting useful sender information. We therefore propose PACT-Check to quantify how much of a trained PACT system's gain genuinely comes from communication. Finally, we discuss the opportunities and challenges of PACT systems. Code is available at https://github.com/zhenyi4/platour.

</details>


### [97] [Follow the Policy, Fail the Benchmark: Auditing and Revising Policy-Based Decision Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07042)
*Yanru Xiao,Yifei Cai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文审计九个基于规则的决策基准，发现参考答案和评分器错误会显著扭曲模型评估，并提出版本化审计与修订集合 BAR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基准自身可能误述规则要求，导致正确遵循规则的回答被判错，进而影响模型比较的可靠性。

Method: 通过追溯规则来源、执行程序和开展受控检查审计九个公开基准；在不改变模型回答的情况下重新评分，并检验中间答案错误对最终决策的影响。

Result: 修正 LegalBench SARA 数值题评分器后，三个模型各自在96道题上的接受率均提高超过80个百分点；RuleArena Tax 恢复必要的零下限后，300个参考答案中有27个改变，观察到的模型差距缩小。受控实验还表明，错误的中间答案可能影响最终决策。

Conclusion: 基准的参考答案、评分器和中间推理步骤均需独立审计；BAR 提供版本化的审计证据与修订，支持重新评估。

Abstract: Policy-based decision benchmarks evaluate whether systems apply written rules to financial and operational cases. Yet a rule-following response can be marked wrong when the benchmark misstates the required decision. We audit nine public policy-based decision benchmarks through source tracing, program execution, and controlled checks. We find unsupported targets, reference errors that survive replay or propagate as solved steps, and evaluators that reject acceptable answers or credit incomplete decisions. To measure the scoring impact, we rescore unchanged model responses. On the 96 LegalBench SARA numeric cases, correcting the scorer raises acceptance by more than 80 percentage points for each of three models, without changing references or responses. In RuleArena Tax, restoring a required zero floor changes 27 of 300 references and narrows an observed model gap. A controlled federal-exemption task with explicit ownership and eligibility assumptions also shows that errors in supplied intermediate answers can affect final decisions. We present BAR (Benchmark Audit and Revision), a versioned collection of audit evidence and revisions for reevaluation.

</details>


### [98] [Reclassification Is All You Missed: Surfacing Variant Reinterpretation Need in Online Health Communities at Population Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07054)
*Kamalakkannan Ravi,Mengshu Nie,Annapurna Poduri,Cong Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PRISM利用大语言模型分析在线健康社区帖子，识别可能涉及遗传变异重新分类的讨论，并草拟患者提醒；初步结果显示事实准确不等于适合临床发送。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遗传变异在患者检测后可能被重新分类，但患者重新联系机制不一致、责任主体不明确，常规临床渠道也可能无法覆盖在线社区中的受影响人群。

Method: 构建智能体式大语言模型流程，筛查32个社区的390万条帖子，核查相关变异是否被重新分类，并生成带免责声明的外联草稿；研究仅进行无实际联系的模拟测试。

Result: 模型预测相关讨论占0.288%；专业遗传学社区为6.30%，普通医疗咨询社区为0.10%，相关讨论自2014年以来增长五倍。系统发现123个候选分类差异，其中5个从意义未明变为致病或可能致病。30份真实帖子草稿中，临床医生认为27份事实有据、15份适合发送。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了群体规模的重新解读需求图谱，但临床可接受的外联不能仅依赖事实准确性；代码和数据计划在论文发表时发布。

Abstract: Genetic variants are routinely reclassified in ClinVar after a patient is tested, sometimes from uncertain to pathogenic, yet patient recontact is inconsistent, with no clear duty and responsibility diffused among labs, providers, and patients. Many affected people discuss their results in public online health communities that clinical recontact often does not reach. We built PRISM, an agentic large-language-model pipeline that screens these posts, checks whether the variant was later reclassified, and drafts a disclaimer-bearing outreach message, in a no-contact dry run. Across 3.9 million posts from 32 communities, agent-predicted reinterpretation-relevant discussion is rare (0.288%) but concentrated in specialist genetics communities (6.30% versus 0.10% in general medical-advice communities) and growing fivefold since 2014; PRISM surfaces 123 candidate classification differences, 5 of them from an uncertain submission to pathogenic or likely pathogenic. On a held-out set of 30 real-post drafts, a clinician judged 27 factually grounded and 15 fit to send: factual accuracy is necessary but not sufficient for clinically acceptable outreach. PRISM contributes a population-scale reinterpretation-need map with code and data to be released upon publication.

</details>


### [99] [Automated Feedback to Peer Tutors Increases Their Talk Moves and Learner Reasoning but Not the Balance of Talk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07072)
*Dorottya Demszky,Yann Hicke,Joy Yun,Mariah Olson,Max Karpawich,Anees Iqbal,Matt Wu,Jimmy Huang,Akshay Ravikumar,Drew Bent*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 自动化反馈改善了志愿导师引导学生思考的教学行为，但未增加学生发言时间；向学生额外提供反馈也未带来进一步改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究已在其他教学场景中显示效果的课堂话语自动化反馈，能否改善小组志愿辅导中的互动，以及向学习者提供反馈能否进一步促进参与。

Method: 在Schoolhouse.world平台的两期、各四周SAT数学集训营中开展随机对照试验，涉及1,030名志愿导师、7,443名学习者和6,508节录制课程。导师被随机分配至无反馈、仅导师反馈，或导师反馈加学习者参与反馈组；学习者反馈采用两种激励框架。研究还访谈了17名导师和13名学习者。

Result: 导师反馈使引导学习者表达观点、回应其贡献及学习者推理等指标提高8%—15%，且在两期集训中均得到重复验证。对照组导师平均发言时间占比为91.5%，各实验条件均未改变这一比例；学习者反馈未进一步改变话语表现。访谈显示，导师尝试采用建议行为，但受到学习者不回应的限制；学习者反馈可能因仅呈现小组统计、部分学习者偏好听讲及同伴互动稀少而效果有限。

Conclusion: 自动化反馈能够改善导师邀请学习者思考的方式，但要增加学习者发言时间，可能需要反馈之外的干预。

Abstract: Automated feedback on classroom discourse improves instruction in professional and online teaching contexts, and recent work suggests that delivering similar feedback to learners may strengthen these effects by sharing responsibility for participation. Whether this holds in small-group volunteer tutoring has not been tested. We present results from a randomized controlled trial across two four-week SAT mathematics bootcamps on the Schoolhouse.world platform, involving 1,030 volunteer tutors, 7,443 learners and 6,508 recorded sessions. Tutors were randomly assigned to receive no feedback, automated feedback on their own discourse, or that feedback together with participation feedback delivered to their learners under one of two motivational framings. Feedback to tutors increased eliciting of learner ideas, uptake of learner contributions, and learner reasoning by 8-15%, and the pattern replicated across both bootcamps. However, tutor talk time, which averaged 91.5% in the control group, did not change in any condition, and learner-facing feedback did not further change discourse. Interviews with 17 tutors indicate that they adopted the moves the feedback recommended but were limited by learners who did not respond. Interviews with 13 learners suggest why the learner-facing feedback had little effect: it reported a group-level rather than a learner-level statistic, some learners preferred to listen, and learner-to-learner interaction was rare in these sessions. Automated feedback can change how tutors invite learner thinking, but shifting how much learners talk likely requires changes beyond feedback.

</details>


### [100] [Consistent yet Contextual: Learning Author-Level Mixtures of Style for Authorship Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07111)
*Omkar Yadav,Michael Jiang,Kenan Alkiek,Jian Zhu,David Jurgens*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 作者身份识别应以多种稳定但不同的写作风格表征作者，并考虑不同情境中文本的作者辨识度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有作者身份验证与归属方法主要建模文档风格，较少直接建模作者及其风格多样性。

Method: 提出多种作者建模方案，以多种一致但不同的风格表征作者；分析受众规模、距模型训练的时间、前文语境和体裁规范四种情境。

Result: 多风格作者表征显著提升识别性能；四类实验均显示，文本体现作者个人独特风格的程度存在明显差异。

Conclusion: 作者身份验证与归属应纳入作者的多种风格，并重视数据情境对文本作者辨识度的影响；摘要未提供具体数据集或量化结果。

Abstract: Approaches to authorship verification or attribution typically model the style of a document, asking whether two documents were likely written by the same author. While recent approaches have been performant, such approaches largely ignore modeling the authors themselves and how varied their style is. Here, we introduce multiple alternatives for modeling the author and demonstrate that allowing an author's representation to capture multiple consistent but distinct writing styles yields significant performance improvements. Using our new authorship model, we then examine when an author writes in a way that is most idiomatic of their style across four scenarios: (i) variation in the size of their writing audiences, (ii) temporal distance from when the model was trained, (iii) the effect of preceding context, and (iv) the effect of genre norms on style. Across all four experiments, we demonstrate that authors vary substantially in how idiolectic their writing is. Our results have clear implications for both the modeling and data used for authorship verification and attribution, showing that authors need to be represented with their multiple styles and that some settings produce text that is much more informative about who wrote that text.

</details>


### [101] [What Patients Say They Can't Access: Mining Cancer Screening Barriers from Online Communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07113)
*Ruimin Sun,Aokun Chen,Arjun Arunasalam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究分析六个癌症相关 Reddit 社区的患者帖子，归纳影响美国患者癌症筛查的结构性、信息性和心理社会障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 癌症筛查参与差异持续存在，传统临床调查难以充分捕捉患者自发分享的现实障碍。

Method: 从六个癌症相关社区的逾5.6万条帖子中，经关键词筛选和障碍分流，确定576条来自美国的帖子，再通过开放式编码和编码手册综合进行定性分析。

Result: 发现反复出现的结构性、信息性和心理社会障碍，且障碍随癌症类型和社区背景而有明显差异；摘要未提供具体障碍或量化效应。

Conclusion: 患者自述可为精准干预、患者导航服务及卫生系统政策提供依据；未来应扩大癌症类型和人群覆盖，并探索规模化识别与应对障碍。

Abstract: Cancer screening disparities persist across the U.S., yet the patient-reported barriers driving low uptake remain poorly characterized at scale. While clinical surveys capture structured responses, they miss the organic, unsolicited discourse where patients candidly describe real-world obstacles. In this paper, we conduct a large-scale analysis of over 56,000 posts across six cancer-specific Reddit communities to surface barriers to cancer screening among U.S. patients. We apply a pipeline combining keyword filtering, barrier triage, open coding, and codebook synthesis to systematically extract and categorize patient-reported barriers from 576 U.S.-located posts. Our qualitative analysis reveals recurring structural, informational, and psychosocial barriers that vary meaningfully by cancer type and community context. These findings lay the groundwork for future work aimed at proactively identifying and addressing barriers at scale - spanning a broader range of cancer types and populations - and informing the design of targeted interventions, navigator programs, and health system policies that translate patient-reported obstacles into actionable solutions.

</details>


### [102] [Adapting LLM Judges to Observed Human Annotation Policy through Soft Prompt Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07126)
*Tamás Janusko,Sven Kleinkop,Ricardo Bochnia,Gunnar Hempel,Steffen Tomschke,Jürgen Anke,Maik Thiele*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 混合软提示调优提升了开源大语言模型评审与人工标注的一致性；调优后的 Qwen 3.5 9B 在三个评估维度上均超过两个托管参考评审模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专有评审模型虽与人工标注一致性较高，但成本和部署门槛较高；轻量开源模型更易部署，却通常与人工评分的一致性较低。

Method: 在 FairBotBench 上训练软提示，将文本提示与可学习的连续提示嵌入结合，对开源评审模型进行混合软提示调优，并从偏见、毒性和个性化三个维度评估。

Result: 混合软提示调优相较对应的未调优基线，持续提高了与人工标注的一致性；调优后的 Qwen 3.5 9B 在三个维度上均优于两个托管参考评审模型。摘要未报告具体数值或统计显著性。

Conclusion: 混合软提示调优有望以较低部署门槛改善开源评审模型对人工标注准则的适配；其跨数据集泛化能力及实际成本优势仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly employed as automated judges for evaluating chatbot responses. While proprietary models can achieve high agreement with human annotators, they remain costly and difficult to deploy in practical settings. Lightweight open-source LLM judges provide an attractive alternative but typically exhibit lower agreement with human ratings. In this paper, we investigate whether soft prompt tuning can align open-source LLM judges more closely with human annotation policy. We train soft prompts on the FairBotBench benchmark and evaluate the adapted judges across the dimensions of bias, toxicity, and personalization. Our findings demonstrate that hybrid soft-prompt tuning, which combines textual prompts with learned continuous prompt embeddings, consistently improves agreement with human annotations over the corresponding untuned baselines. For Qwen 3.5 9B, the adapted judges surpass both hosted reference judges across all three evaluation dimensions.

</details>


### [103] [Matriarchy Classics: A Computational Approach to Swap Only Gendered Words in Classic Novels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07134)
*Rachel Lea Draelos*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: This paper proposes a trustworthy computational method to gender-swap public domain novels, with programmatic guarantees that only gendered words are inverted and all other words are left unchanged. This may seem like a simple task, but both large language models and naive rule-based methods fail to complete it. The proposed method includes a custom whitespace-preserving tokenizer, gender-swap mappings across plural and possessive forms, pronoun handling, and a character renaming strategy. It shortens the time required to gender-swap a novel from weeks of manual labor to minutes. The method is demonstrated on two public-domain novels. Pride and Prejudice is transported to matriarchal Regency England, where inheritance flows through daughters, mothers rule the household, and a frantic Mr. Bennet is determined to secure advantageous marriages for his sons. The Great Gatsby pivots to a matriarchal Jazz Age where newly-rich Ms. Gatsbee throws lavish parties in the hopes of reuniting with her lost love, David, who is now married to the violent Thelma Buchanan. Gender-swapped novels could serve as useful tools to investigate the gender biases of readers, authors, societies, and AI systems.

</details>


### [104] [τ-bench-LILT: Evaluating Conversational Agents on Realistic Multilingual Tool-Use Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07203)
*Kaden Uhlig,Kiarash Mokhtari,Anıl Arslan,Yunsu Kim,Joern Wuebker*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: τ-bench-LILT 将工具调用型对话智能体基准扩展至四种语言；部分非英语零售任务的 pass^4 较英语最多下降 61 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前沿评测几乎只使用英语，难以衡量智能体在其他语言和地区部署时的表现。

Method: 在航空、零售、电信和银行领域支持德语、韩语、土耳其语和波斯语；本地化客户数据库，并由母语专家通过对比标注迭代优化用户模拟器提示词，再根据本地数据和实际需求调整任务。

Result: 对八个智能体模型的评测显示，韩语和波斯语零售领域相较英语的性能降幅最大，pass^4 最多下降 61 个百分点。

Conclusion: 非英语场景存在显著的智能体性能差距，需要采用全面本地化的多语言基准进行评测；代码和数据已公开。

Abstract: Frontier evaluations of tool-using conversational agents are conducted almost exclusively in English, leaving a critical gap for organizations deploying agents in other languages and regions. We present $τ$-bench-LILT, a multilingual extension of ${τ^3}$-bench covering German, Korean, Turkish, and Farsi in the airline, retail, telecom, and banking domains. We natively localize every layer of the benchmark: customer databases are populated with culturally appropriate records, user-simulator prompts are refined iteratively through contrastive annotation by native-language experts, and tasks are updated according to the localized database and to reflect realistic tasks in the target region. Evaluation across eight agent models shows the largest drops compared to English in the Korean and Farsi retail domains, by up to 61 points in pass^4. Code and data released at https://github.com/lilt/tau-bench-lilt.

</details>


### [105] [SAFE-Teen: Evaluating Chatbot Safety Across Adolescent Mental Health Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07218)
*Andrea Mock,Valerie Chen,Eric Lin,Rayan Krishnan,Diyi Yang,Caroline Figueroa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 对青少年与聊天机器人模拟对话的评估发现，青少年专用系统提示可降低安全标准失败率，但不能消除风险；青少年的回应方式也会影响安全表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 青少年日益使用聊天机器人寻求心理健康支持、建议和陪伴，但现有研究较少比较同一情境下青少年回应的细微变化如何影响安全性。

Method: 在临床专业知识指导下，评估18个聊天机器人API在两种系统提示条件下的32,555段模拟对话；使用22项安全标准，覆盖106个种子情境的983种变体，模拟对象为13—17岁青少年。

Result: 未使用和使用青少年专用提示时，总体安全标准失败率分别为43.3%和24.9%。未使用该提示时，若青少年对回答提出异议，超过80%的聊天机器人在心理健康诊断、药物及物质滥用相关边界上的失败率上升10—12个百分点。

Conclusion: 青少年专用提示能够降低但无法消除模拟对话中的安全失败；安全性取决于对话的发展过程，评估应覆盖多样化的用户回应。

Abstract: Adolescents are increasingly turning to AI chatbots for mental health support, advice, and companionship, raising safety concerns. While recent evaluations have started to examine safety across multiple conversational turns, few compare how slight changes in a teen's responses can affect a chatbot's safety in the same scenario. We conduct a clinician-informed evaluation of chatbot safety in simulated conversations with adolescents aged 13-17, examining how safety failures vary with adolescents' responses and teen-aware system prompting. We evaluated 32,555 simulated conversations across 18 chatbot APIs and two system-prompt conditions using 22 safety criteria grounded in previously identified harms. Conversations spanned 983 variants of 106 seed scenarios covering urgent safety issues, health advice, and identity and relationships. The overall criterion failure rate was 43.3% without the teen-aware prompt and 24.9% with it. The teen-aware prompt reduced failures, but it didn't eliminate them. When a teenager pushed back on a chatbot's answer, failures on mental health diagnosis, medication, and substance misuse boundaries rose by 10-12 percentage points without the prompt, for more than 80% of chatbots. These findings reveal persistent safety failures in simulated adolescent-chatbot interactions and show that safety depends on how a conversation unfolds.

</details>


### [106] [Source-balanced Capped Fisher: Bounded Evidence Aggregation for User Inference in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07228)
*Yang Liu,Ran Zou,Wanrong Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出源均衡截断 Fisher（SCF）方法，利用未参与源构造参考分布并校准检验，以识别数据源是否参与语言模型更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 审计者只能获取数据源的新文档；文档间基线可预测性及其对同源已学习信息的反映程度不同，使文档级证据难以可靠聚合。

Method: 使用已知未参与源评估文档分数的参考尾部频率，转换为负对数证据，对单文档贡献截断并在源内取平均；再用另一组未参与源校准 p 值，判断源是否参与。

Result: 在四个真实数据集对应的四个领域中，SCF 的 AUROC 均为所评估聚合方法中最高；校准后的检测功效总体具有竞争力，多种设置下最高，经验假阳性率通常接近或低于预设水平。

Conclusion: SCF 可稳健聚合多文档证据；在数据源可交换的条件下，校准决策规则可提供有限样本第一类错误率控制。

Abstract: User inference asks whether a source contributed documents to a language-model update, even when the auditor observes only fresh documents from that source. Document-level scores can provide evidence of participation, but combining this evidence is challenging: documents differ in baseline predictability and in how strongly they reflect information learned from other documents of the same source. We propose source-balanced capped Fisher (SCF), an aggregation method that uses known nonparticipating sources to assess how unusual each document score is. It transforms the resulting reference-tail frequencies into negative-log evidence, caps individual contributions, and averages them within each source. This limits the influence of isolated extreme scores while retaining evidence across multiple documents. We complete the inference procedure with a decision rule that tests source participation using a p-value calibrated against a separate set of known nonparticipating sources. The rule provides finite-sample Type-I error control under source exchangeability. Experiments across four real-world datasets show that SCF achieves the highest AUROC among the evaluated aggregation methods in four domains. With the calibration-based decision rule, SCF delivers consistently competitive detection power across the tested settings, achieving the highest power in many cases, while empirical false-positive rates remain generally close to or below the nominal levels.

</details>


### [107] [Recursive Sparse Attention: Million-Token Length Extrapolation at Logarithmic Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07232)
*Ruitian Wang,Xinyu Yang,Minshen Zhang,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RecSA 使用递归树形稀疏注意力，将每个查询的选择和读取成本降至 O(log L)，并实现从 32K 训练长度向百万级上下文的泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 百万 token 上下文中的稠密注意力计算成本高；长文档训练数据稀缺。多数稀疏方法仍需 O(L) 选择成本，既有可训练层次方法缺少超训练长度的准确率验证。

Method: 在 KV 缓存上构建树，每个节点以键和均值池化值概括子树；各层共享跨树层级的键池化器，通过稠密注意力蒸馏训练。每层最多选择 k 个候选，未选候选的摘要与精确 token 共同参与 softmax；使用层内候选索引作为 RoPE 位置，限制相对距离。

Result: 基于 Qwen3-4B，在 32K 长度上训练 0.62B token；RULER 测试中，131K 至 2M 各长度均优于基线，1M 时得分 60.5，最佳基线 Dense + DCA 为 40.0。单张 H200、524K 长度下，预填充和解码分别加速 3.2 倍和 3.8 倍。

Conclusion: 递归摘要、层次选择和位置编码设计相结合，可在降低长上下文注意力成本的同时支持超训练长度泛化；结果基于摘要所述模型和评测设置。

Abstract: At context lengths of about 1M tokens, dense attention is costly in prefill and decoding, and training typically relies on long documents, which become scarcer as the target length increases. Most sparse attention methods score all preceding blocks or tokens, so their selection cost per query is $O(L)$ at context length $L$; hierarchical selection reduces it to $O(\log L)$, but prior trainable hierarchical methods report accuracy only within their training length. We propose RecSA (Recursive Sparse Attention), which builds a tree over the KV cache in which each node summarizes its subtree with one key and one mean-pooled value. The keys come from one pooler per layer, shared by all levels and distilled from dense attention. Each level above the tokens selects at most $k$ candidates, so each query has $O(\log L)$ selection cost and reads $O(\log L)$ entries; the summaries of unselected candidates share one softmax with the exact tokens. Each entry's RoPE position is its index among the candidates of its level, so at any length every relative distance stays within the range seen during training. Initialized from Qwen3-4B and trained on 0.62B tokens at the original model's 32K training length, RecSA outperforms all baselines on RULER at every length from 131K to 2M; at 1M, it scores 60.5, compared with 40.0 for the best baseline, Dense + DCA. With our optimized kernels, RecSA is faster than dense attention in end-to-end prefill and decoding from 64K to 2M; at 524K on one H200 GPU, it accelerates prefill by 3.2x and decoding by 3.8x.

</details>


### [108] [Archetypal contexts in the neurons of language models: an architecture for intelligence?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07234)
*David Nordfors*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: We validate our previous hypothesis that a language model holds relational patterns apart from the subject domains that instantiate them (Nordfors, 2026). These archetypal contexts are patterns of relations that people recognize across many fields, such as the tipping point or winner takes all, held once and applied across domains. We show this inside the model, Qwen3.8-27B. One set of neurons carries the archetype, say the tipping point; another carries the topic, say politics, ecology or cell biology; a third carries the language, such as English, Spanish or Chinese. Silence the tipping point's neurons, and the model no longer identifies the pattern but still knows what the text is about. Silence the topic's, and it loses the topic but still names the pattern. In the neuron maps the layers organize themselves into a sandwich: the outer layers carry languages, the layers inside them topics, the layers at the heart archetypes. The sandwich emerges spontaneously during pretraining and appears in every model we mapped. Archetypes are the architecture for analogy, and we strongly suggest that this structure of archetypes and topics is the mechanism of a language model's intelligence: we see it self-organize as the model is trained. The neurons, the texts and the code are released with this paper as its reproduction package, so that anyone can repeat the test inside the model.

</details>


### [109] [How Do AI Agents Waste Your Money?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07238)
*Huimin Zeng,Yuxuan Lu,Jesse Cai,Eric Hanchen Jiang,Di Wu,Po-Nien Kung,Wenbo Hu,Ziyi Wang,Dakuo Wang,Jiaxin Pei,Kai-Wei Chang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出跨智能体系统的 token 浪费测量框架；在线剪枝移除 41%–46% 的模型推理内容，同时基本保持任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体任务可能消耗大量 token，但现有研究缺乏系统识别和量化不同智能体系统中 token 浪费的方法。

Method: 记录并标准化完整智能体轨迹，以大语言模型作为评判器识别可删除文本片段，再映射回原始轨迹进行 token 级测量；通过删除片段、恢复执行并比较任务结果及后续行动，验证所识别的浪费。

Result: 在两个不同的智能体基准上，在线剪枝移除 41%–46% 的模型推理内容，任务表现总体基本保持。浪费程度在不同任务和智能体配置间的差异大于模型间差异；推理与可见执行的浪费模式可能不同；即使模型和任务相同，不同智能体运行框架也会引入不同低效来源。

Conclusion: token 浪费具有仅凭总体 token 用量无法揭示的系统性结构；该框架可将浪费转化为可操作的优化信号，支持改进模型训练、推理策略和智能体运行框架。

Abstract: AI agents may consume massive tokens across a wide range of tasks, but how many of those tokens are really necessary? Although prior work has shown that agents can incur unexpectedly high token costs, we still lack a systematic way to identify and measure token waste. In this work, we introduce a unified framework for measuring token waste across heterogeneous agent systems. Our framework records and normalizes complete agent trajectories, identifies removable spans using an LLM-as-a-Judge (LLMaJ), and maps the identified text spans back to the original trajectory for token-level measurement. We validate the identified waste through direct interventions by removing marked spans, resuming agent execution, and measuring changes in task outcomes and subsequent actions. In online pruning experiments, we remove 41-46% of the model's reasoning while largely preserving task performance across two distinct agent benchmarks. Using this framework, we analyze agents across multiple settings. Our analysis shows that token waste has a system-level structure that aggregate token usage alone cannot reveal. First, waste varies more across tasks and agent settings than across models. Second, reasoning and visible execution can exhibit different waste patterns. Third, different harnesses can introduce distinct sources of inefficiency even under the same model and task. Overall, our framework transforms token waste into an actionable optimization signal, enabling researchers and practitioners to train more efficient models, design better reasoning strategies, and optimize agent harnesses toward reducing unnecessary computation rather than merely reducing token usage.

</details>


### [110] [HyFlash: A Prefix-Memory Prior for On-Device Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07263)
*Jongwoo Ko,Pashmina Cameron*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HyFlash 通过局部注意力与固定大小的循环记忆相结合，在降低推测解码草稿侧内存开销的同时，提高草稿接受率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端侧设备的内存限制了草稿模型容量和上下文存储；全上下文注意力的 KV 缓存随上下文增长，难以兼顾长程依赖与草稿位置间的细粒度交互。

Method: 提出紧凑型并行草稿模型 HyFlash，引入前缀记忆先验：局部注意力处理近期上下文及草稿位置交互，循环记忆将历史目标模型特征压缩为固定大小状态。

Result: 实验表明，相比全上下文注意力，HyFlash 使用显著更少的 KV 存储并获得更高接受率；优势在浅层草稿模型和长上下文中更明显。摘要未给出具体数值。

Conclusion: 将长程上下文建模与局部细粒度交互分离，可在有界草稿侧内存下改善端侧推测解码的效率。

Abstract: Parallel block drafting accelerates speculative decoding by predicting multiple future positions in a single backbone pass, but on-device deployment constrains both drafter capacity and context storage. Under tight memory budgets, drafters must model long-range dependencies and fine-grained draft-position interactions without the growing KV-cache overhead of full-context attention. We propose HyFlash, a compact parallel drafter that introduces a prefix-memory prior to separate long-range context modeling from fine-grained draft-position interactions with limited capacity. Local attention captures recent context and draft-position interactions, while recurrent memory compresses historical target features into fixed-size states. This inductive bias enables effective local and long-range modeling with bounded draft-side memory. Experiments show that HyFlash achieves higher acceptance than full-context attention with substantially less KV storage and with larger gains for shallow drafters and long contexts.

</details>


### [111] [Encoding and Propagation of Grammatical Number in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07283)
*Jacob Solinsky,Serguei Pakhomov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 利用稀疏自编码器分析语言模型中的英语数一致性，并通过因果干预发现复数特征比相应单数特征更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解大语言模型如何编码和处理语法信息，并探索稀疏自编码器在机制可解释性和语言表征研究中的用途。

Method: 在 Pythia-70M 上训练稀疏自编码器，追踪复数名词、复数主句主语和复数动词生成等特征；干预主语词元的隐藏状态，观察动词数形式的变化，并在更大模型上复现。

Result: 移除复数主语的相关特征可促使模型生成单数动词，反向干预亦可改变输出；对应单数特征的干预效果较弱。Gemma-2-2B 和 Llama-3.1-8B 上获得类似结果。

Conclusion: 结果支持模型可能采用缺项式语法数表征：复数具有更明确的特征标记，而单数相对缺乏对应标记。论文还提出了应用稀疏自编码器研究语言表征的方法。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) have become an increasingly popular means of translating Large Language Model (LLM) hidden state vectors into a human-comprehensible list of features or symbols. As such, SAEs may provide insight into an LLM's knowledge representation and internal ``mental'' grammar - topics of pragmatic interest for mechanistic interpretation of AI behavior and of theoretical interest for the study of in silico representations of human language. Language models with fewer than 100M parameters, trained on small corpora, nonetheless exhibit strong grammatical competence and discourse coherence, despite producing content of limited reliability. We use SAEs that we trained on one such model (Pythia-70M) to conduct an in-depth examination of how a single grammatical phenomenon, number agreement, is represented and processed in an LLM. We demonstrate the mechanism and the methodology by which SAEs can be used to find and propagate ``plural noun'', ``plural matrix subject'', and ``generate a plural verb'' features which cause the LLM to produce plural verb forms such as ``are'', ``do'', or ``have'' instead of ``is'', ``does'', and ``has'' in response to prompts such as ``the men in the park''. We show that intervening to remove these features from the hidden states of plural subject noun tokens causes the model to produce singular verbs instead, and vice versa. We also show that corresponding ``singular noun'' and ``generate a singular verb'' features are not as effective for this task. This asymmetry suggests that Pythia-70m makes use of a privative representation of grammatical number in English. These findings replicated with larger LLMs Gemma-2-2b and Llama-3.1-8b yielded similar results. Our main contribution is a novel approach to the use of SAEs to understanding how LLMs encode human language that addresses a number of technical and methodological challenges.

</details>


### [112] [Latency-Aware Adaptive Best-of-$N$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07453)
*Alon Jacobson,Qian Xie,Peter I. Frazier*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种感知延迟的两阶段自适应 Best-of-N 采样方法，以更低的综合成本获得与固定 Best-of-N 相当的奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线推理的成本不仅包括生成次数，还包括随服务器负载变化的延迟；固定奖励阈值无法适应生成速度的变化。

Method: 先生成第一批样本，依据最高奖励和实测延迟比较第二批的预期收益与预测成本，决定是否追加采样及其批量大小。利用提示词奖励分布的累积分布函数归一化奖励，并通过离线回归预测首批最高奖励的归一化分布，以闭式计算预期收益。

Result: 在基于偏好的对齐基准上进行模拟，以生成次数加折算后的延迟衡量成本。该方法以更低综合成本达到 Best-of-N 的奖励；利用首批延迟至少不逊于固定阈值基线，在延迟高度可变、随批量增长或两批延迟高度相关时节省更多。

Conclusion: 根据实测延迟动态调整推理计算量，可改善在线服务中的奖励与综合成本权衡；证据目前来自模拟实验。

Abstract: Test-time compute (TTC) improves language model responses by spending additional computation at inference time, and the right amount of compute depends on the prompt. Adaptive TTC methods allocate compute based on signals of response quality such as observed rewards, often by stopping once the signal exceeds a fixed threshold, and generally measure cost by the number of generations or tokens. But in online serving, latency is also a cost: it grows with server load, so a method using a fixed threshold spends too little compute when generation is fast and too much when it is slow. For Best-of-$N$ sampling, a widely used TTC method, we propose a two-stage adaptive algorithm that draws an initial batch, then uses the observed rewards and the batch's latency to decide whether to draw a second batch and at what size by comparing expected improvement against predicted cost: it draws one when the first batch's best reward is below a threshold that decreases with the first batch's latency, without explicitly measuring server load. We map each reward to its value under the cumulative distribution function (CDF) of the prompt's reward distribution, so the same tradeoff parameters can be reused across tasks; a regression fitted offline on other prompts predicts a distribution for the CDF value of the first batch's best reward, giving the expected improvement in closed form.
